+import { randomBytes } from "node:crypto";
+import {
+  mkdirSync, mkdtempSync,
+  readFileSync, rmSync, symlinkSync, writeFileSync,
+} from "node:fs";
+import { homedir, tmpdir } from "node:os";
+import { dirname, join } from "node:path";
+import { encodeSeed } from "ripple-address-codec";
+import {
+  CITED_VECTORS, loadStore, pathHits, scanRepo, scanText,
+} from "./check-no-secrets.ts";
+import { scrub } from "./scrub-personal-data.ts";
+
+const ROOT = join(import.meta.dir, "..");
+const GATE = join(import.meta.dir, "check-no-secrets.ts");
+const scratch = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), "review056-"));
+const gitEnv = {
+  GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM: "1",
+  GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL: "/dev/null",
+  GIT_AUTHOR_NAME: "probe",
+  GIT_AUTHOR_EMAIL: "probe@example.invalid",
+  GIT_COMMITTER_NAME: "probe",
+  GIT_COMMITTER_EMAIL: "probe@example.invalid",
+};
+
+function run(cmd: string[], cwd = ROOT, input?: string, env = gitEnv) {
+  const r = Bun.spawnSync(cmd, { cwd, ...(input === undefined ? {} : { stdin: new TextEncoder().encode(input) }), stdout: "pipe", stderr: "pipe", env: { ...process.env, ...env } });
+  return {
+    exit: r.exitCode ?? -1,
+    out: new TextDecoder().decode(r.stdout),
+    err: new TextDecoder().decode(r.stderr),
+  };
+}
+
+function g(repo: string, ...args: string[]) {
+  const r = run(["git", ...args], repo);
+  if (r.exit !== 0) throw new Error(`git ${args.join(" ")} failed: ${r.err}`);
+  return r.out.trim();
+}
+
+function init(repo: string) {
+  mkdirSync(repo, { recursive: true });
+  g(repo, "init", "-q", "-b", "main");
+}
+
+function put(repo: string, rel: string, body: string) {
+  const p = join(repo, rel);
+  mkdirSync(dirname(p), { recursive: true });
+  writeFileSync(p, body);
+}
+
+function gate(args: string[]) {
+  const r = run(["bun", "run", GATE, ...args], ROOT);
+  const lines = r.out.trimEnd().split("\n");
+  return { exit: r.exit, last: lines.at(-1) ?? "", scanned: lines.find((l) => l.startsWith("scanned:")) ?? "" };
+}
+
+const emptyStore = { files: [], needles: [], secrets: 0, publicSkipped: 0 };
+const scalar64 = "a".repeat(64);
+const scalar63 = "a".repeat(63);
+
+try {
+  console.log("DETECTOR SHAPES");
+  const shapes: [string, string][] = [
+    ["63-digit labelled scalar", `private_key: ${scalar63}`],
+    ["pretty JSON scalar on next line", `{"private_key":\n  "${scalar64}"}`],
+    ["Markdown value before label", `| ${scalar64} | private key |`],
+    ["pretty JSON seed entropy on next line", `{"seed":\n  "${randomBytes(16).toString("hex")}"}`],
+    ["Windows forward-slash home", "C:[synthetic personal-path redacted]/work"],
+    ["AWS secret access key", `aws_secret_access_key=${"A".repeat(40)}`],
+  ];
+  for (const [name, text] of shapes) console.log(`${name}: findings=${scanText(text, emptyStore).filter((h) => !h.allowedBy).length}`);
+
+  const toy = `private_key: ${"0".repeat(63)}7`;
+  const publicSeed = encodeSeed(Buffer.alloc(16, 0x42), "ed25519");
+  const toyHits = scanText(toy, emptyStore);
+  const publicHits = scanText(`production_seed=${publicSeed}`, emptyStore);
+  console.log(`toy scalar: findings=${toyHits.filter((h) => !h.allowedBy).length} allowed=${toyHits.some((h) => h.allowedBy?.includes("toy scalar"))}`);
+  console.log(`repeated-byte production seed: findings=${publicHits.filter((h) => !h.allowedBy).length} allowed=${publicHits.some((h) => h.allowedBy?.includes("public entropy"))}`);
+
+  const storeDir = join(scratch, "malformed-store");
+  mkdirSync(storeDir);
+  const storedSeed = encodeSeed(randomBytes(16), "ed25519");
+  const omittedScalar = randomBytes(32).toString("hex");
+  writeFileSync(join(storeDir, "good.json"), JSON.stringify({ seed: storedSeed }));
+  writeFileSync(join(storeDir, "broken.json"), `{"sk":"${omittedScalar}"`);
+  const malformedStore = loadStore([storeDir], {});
+  console.log(`malformed store: secrets=${malformedStore.secrets} files=${malformedStore.files.length} bare-scalar-findings=${scanText(omittedScalar, malformedStore).filter((h) => !h.allowedBy).length}`);
+
+  console.log("\nENUMERATION");
+  const filterRepo = join(scratch, "filter-repo");
+  init(filterRepo);
+  const filteredSeed = encodeSeed(randomBytes(16), "ed25519");
+  put(filterRepo, ".gitattributes", "payload.txt filter=inject\n");
+  put(filterRepo, "payload.txt", "SAFE\n");
+  put(filterRepo, "cited.txt", CITED_VECTORS.map((v) => `private_key: ${v.value}`).join("\n") + "\n");
+  g(filterRepo, "config", "filter.inject.clean", `sed s/SAFE/${filteredSeed}/g`);
+  const beforeAdd = gate(["--repo", filterRepo, "--tree-only", "--no-local-store"]);
+  g(filterRepo, "add", "-A");
+  g(filterRepo, "commit", "-q", "--no-gpg-sign", "-m", "filtered");
+  const committedFiltered = g(filterRepo, "show", "HEAD:payload.txt").includes(filteredSeed);
+  const afterAdd = gate(["--repo", filterRepo, "--rev", "HEAD", "--no-local-store"]);
+  console.log(`clean filter before git add: ${beforeAdd.last}`);
+  console.log(`clean filter committed secret=${committedFiltered}; after commit: ${afterAdd.last}`);
+
+  const replaceRepo = join(scratch, "replace-repo");
+  init(replaceRepo);
+  const replacedSeed = encodeSeed(randomBytes(16), "secp256k1");
+  put(replaceRepo, "leak.txt", replacedSeed + "\n");
+  g(replaceRepo, "add", "-A");
+  g(replaceRepo, "commit", "-q", "--no-gpg-sign", "-m", "secret object");
+  const oldBlob = g(replaceRepo, "rev-parse", "HEAD:leak.txt");
+  const cleanBlob = run(["git", "hash-object", "-w", "--stdin"], replaceRepo, "SAFE\n").out.trim();
+  g(replaceRepo, "replace", oldBlob, cleanBlob);
+  writeFileSync(join(replaceRepo, "leak.txt"), "SAFE\n");
+  g(replaceRepo, "replace", "-d", oldBlob);
+  const original = g(replaceRepo, "cat-file", "blob", oldBlob).includes(replacedSeed);
+  g(replaceRepo, "replace", oldBlob, cleanBlob);
+  const revReplace = gate(["--repo", replaceRepo, "--rev", "HEAD", "--no-local-store"]);
+  const histReplace = gate(["--repo", replaceRepo, "--history-only", "--no-local-store"]);
+  console.log(`replace ref original secret=${original}; rev: ${revReplace.last}; history: ${histReplace.last}`);
+
+  const tabRepo = join(scratch, "tab-repo");
+  init(tabRepo);
+  put(tabRepo, "safe\t/Users/Alice/state.json", "SAFE\n");
+  g(tabRepo, "add", "-A");
+  g(tabRepo, "commit", "-q", "--no-gpg-sign", "-m", "odd path");
+  const rawPathHasSecretShape = g(tabRepo, "ls-tree", "-r", "--name-only", "HEAD").includes("state.json");
+  const tabRev = gate(["--repo", tabRepo, "--rev", "HEAD", "--no-local-store"]);
+  console.log(`tab filename contains secret path=${rawPathHasSecretShape}; rev: ${tabRev.last}`);
+
+  const sub = join(scratch, "sub");
+  init(sub);
+  put(sub, "ok.txt", "SAFE\n");
+  g(sub, "add", "-A");
+  g(sub, "commit", "-q", "--no-gpg-sign", "-m", "sub");
+  const superRepo = join(scratch, "super");
+  init(superRepo);
+  g(superRepo, "-c", "protocol.file.allow=always", "submodule", "add", "-q", sub, "vendor/sub");
+  g(superRepo, "commit", "-q", "--no-gpg-sign", "-am", "submodule");
+  const subRev = gate(["--repo", superRepo, "--rev", "HEAD", "--no-local-store"]);
+  console.log(`ordinary submodule rev: ${subRev.last}`);
+
+  const histRepo = join(scratch, "history-repo");
+  init(histRepo);
+  put(histRepo, "state.json", "{}\n");
+  g(histRepo, "add", "-A");
+  g(histRepo, "commit", "-q", "--no-gpg-sign", "-m", "old path");
+  g(histRepo, "mv", "state.json", "safe.txt");
+  g(histRepo, "commit", "-q", "--no-gpg-sign", "-m", "rename");
+  const tagSeed = encodeSeed(randomBytes(16), "ed25519");
+  g(histRepo, "tag", "-a", "probe-tag", "-m", `tag carries ${tagSeed}`);
+  g(histRepo, "checkout", "-q", "-b", "side", "HEAD~1");
+  put(histRepo, "side.txt", "SAFE\n");
+  g(histRepo, "add", "-A");
+  g(histRepo, "commit", "-q", "--no-gpg-sign", "-m", "side");
+  g(histRepo, "checkout", "-q", "main");
+  const mergeSeed = encodeSeed(randomBytes(16), "secp256k1");
+  g(histRepo, "merge", "--no-ff", "--no-gpg-sign", "-m", `merge carries ${mergeSeed}`, "side");
+  const mergeSha = g(histRepo, "rev-parse", "HEAD").slice(0, 10);
+  const hr = scanRepo(histRepo, emptyStore, { tree: false, history: true });
+  console.log(`history rename-old-path=${hr.findings.some((f) => f.cls === "secret-file" && f.where.startsWith("state.json"))} annotated-tag=${hr.findings.some((f) => f.cls === "seed" && f.where.startsWith("tag "))} merge-message=${hr.findings.some((f) => f.cls === "seed" && f.where.startsWith(`commit ${mergeSha}`))}`);
+
+  console.log("\nSCRUBBER");
+  const unreported = `X${homedir()}/file`;

+  const winFile = join(scratch, "windows.txt");

+import { mkdtempSync, readFileSync, rmSync, symlinkSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
+import { tmpdir } from "node:os";
+import { join } from "node:path";
+
+const ROOT = join(import.meta.dir, "..");
+const scratch = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), "review056-mutant-"));
+const clone = join(scratch, "repo");
+const env = {
+  ...process.env,
+  GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM: "1",
+  GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL: "/dev/null",
+  GIT_AUTHOR_NAME: "probe",
+  GIT_AUTHOR_EMAIL: "probe@example.invalid",
+  GIT_COMMITTER_NAME: "probe",
+  GIT_COMMITTER_EMAIL: "probe@example.invalid",
+};
+const run = (cmd: string[]) => {
+  const r = Bun.spawnSync(cmd, { cwd: clone, stdout: "pipe", stderr: "pipe", env });
+  return { exit: r.exitCode ?? -1, out: new TextDecoder().decode(r.stdout), err: new TextDecoder().decode(r.stderr) };
+};
+
+try {
+  const c = Bun.spawnSync(["git", "clone", "-q", "--no-hardlinks", ROOT, clone], { stdout: "pipe", stderr: "pipe", env });
+  if (c.exitCode !== 0) throw new Error(new TextDecoder().decode(c.stderr));
+  symlinkSync(join(ROOT, "node_modules"), join(clone, "node_modules"), "dir");
+  const gate = join(clone, "tools", "check-no-secrets.ts");
+  const source = readFileSync(gate, "utf8");
+  const line = '  { name: "OpenAI API key", re: /\\bsk-(?:proj-)?[A-Za-z0-9_-]{40,}/g },\n';
+  writeFileSync(gate, source.replace(line, ""));
+
+  const controls = run(["bun", "run", gate, "--controls-only"]);
+  console.log("controls", controls.exit, controls.out.trimEnd().split("\n").at(-1));
+  const harness = run(["bun", "run", join(clone, "tools", "break-no-secrets-gate.ts")]);
+  console.log("harness", harness.exit);
+  console.log(harness.out.split("\n").filter((l) => l.startsWith("✗") || l.includes("verdict=") || l.includes("baseline: the real tree")).join("\n"));
+  writeFileSync(join(clone, "synthetic-openai-token.txt"), `sk-${"A".repeat(40)}\n`);
+  const leak = run(["bun", "run", gate, "--tree-only", "--no-local-store"]);
+  console.log("leak", leak.exit, leak.out.split("\n").filter((l) => l.startsWith("FAILING") || l.startsWith("  ") || l.startsWith("check-no-secrets:")).slice(-30).join("\n"));
+} finally {
+  rmSync(scratch, { recursive: true, force: true });
+}

+  put(filterRepo, "cited.txt", CITED_VECTORS.map((v) => `private_key: ${v.value}`).join("\n") + "\n");
+  g(filterRepo, "config", "filter.inject.clean", `sed s/SAFE/${filteredSeed}/g`);
+  const beforeAdd = gate(["--repo", filterRepo, "--tree-only", "--no-local-store"]);
+  g(filterRepo, "add", "-A");
+  g(filterRepo, "commit", "-q", "--no-gpg-sign", "-m", "filtered");
+  const committedFiltered = g(filterRepo, "show", "HEAD:payload.txt").includes(filteredSeed);
+  const afterAdd = gate(["--repo", filterRepo, "--rev", "HEAD", "--no-local-store"]);
+  console.log(`clean filter before git add: ${beforeAdd.last}`);
+  console.log(`clean filter committed secret=${committedFiltered}; after commit: ${afterAdd.last}`);
+
+  const replaceRepo = join(scratch, "replace-repo");
+  init(replaceRepo);
+  const replacedSeed = encodeSeed(randomBytes(16), "secp256k1");
+  put(replaceRepo, "leak.txt", replacedSeed + "\n");
+  g(replaceRepo, "add", "-A");
+  g(replaceRepo, "commit", "-q", "--no-gpg-sign", "-m", "secret object");
+  const oldBlob = g(replaceRepo, "rev-parse", "HEAD:leak.txt");
+  const cleanBlob = run(["git", "hash-object", "-w", "--stdin"], replaceRepo, "SAFE\n").out.trim();
+  g(replaceRepo, "replace", oldBlob, cleanBlob);
+  writeFileSync(join(replaceRepo, "leak.txt"), "SAFE\n");
+  g(replaceRepo, "replace", "-d", oldBlob);
+  const original = g(replaceRepo, "cat-file", "blob", oldBlob).includes(replacedSeed);
+  g(replaceRepo, "replace", oldBlob, cleanBlob);
+  const revReplace = gate(["--repo", replaceRepo, "--rev", "HEAD", "--no-local-store"]);
+  const histReplace = gate(["--repo", replaceRepo, "--history-only", "--no-local-store"]);
+  console.log(`replace ref original secret=${original}; rev: ${revReplace.last}; history: ${histReplace.last}`);
+
+  const tabRepo = join(scratch, "tab-repo");
+  init(tabRepo);
+  put(tabRepo, "safe\t/Users/Alice/state.json", "SAFE\n");
+  g(tabRepo, "add", "-A");
+  g(tabRepo, "commit", "-q", "--no-gpg-sign", "-m", "odd path");
+  const rawPathHasSecretShape = g(tabRepo, "ls-tree", "-r", "--name-only", "HEAD").includes("state.json");
+  const tabRev = gate(["--repo", tabRepo, "--rev", "HEAD", "--no-local-store"]);
+  console.log(`tab filename contains secret path=${rawPathHasSecretShape}; rev: ${tabRev.last}`);
+
+  const sub = join(scratch, "sub");
+  init(sub);
+  put(sub, "ok.txt", "SAFE\n");
+  g(sub, "add", "-A");
+  g(sub, "commit", "-q", "--no-gpg-sign", "-m", "sub");
+  const superRepo = join(scratch, "super");
+  init(superRepo);
+  g(superRepo, "-c", "protocol.file.allow=always", "submodule", "add", "-q", sub, "vendor/sub");
+  g(superRepo, "commit", "-q", "--no-gpg-sign", "-am", "submodule");
+  const subRev = gate(["--repo", superRepo, "--rev", "HEAD", "--no-local-store"]);
+  console.log(`ordinary submodule rev: ${subRev.last}`);
+
+  const histRepo = join(scratch, "history-repo");
+  init(histRepo);
+  put(histRepo, "state.json", "{}\n");
+  g(histRepo, "add", "-A");
+  g(histRepo, "commit", "-q", "--no-gpg-sign", "-m", "old path");
+  g(histRepo, "mv", "state.json", "safe.txt");
+  g(histRepo, "commit", "-q", "--no-gpg-sign", "-m", "rename");
+  const tagSeed = encodeSeed(randomBytes(16), "ed25519");
+  g(histRepo, "tag", "-a", "probe-tag", "-m", `tag carries ${tagSeed}`);
+  g(histRepo, "checkout", "-q", "-b", "side", "HEAD~1");
+  put(histRepo, "side.txt", "SAFE\n");
+  g(histRepo, "add", "-A");
+  g(histRepo, "commit", "-q", "--no-gpg-sign", "-m", "side");
+  g(histRepo, "checkout", "-q", "main");
+  const mergeSeed = encodeSeed(randomBytes(16), "secp256k1");
+  g(histRepo, "merge", "--no-ff", "--no-gpg-sign", "-m", `merge carries ${mergeSeed}`, "side");
+  const mergeSha = g(histRepo, "rev-parse", "HEAD").slice(0, 10);
+  const hr = scanRepo(histRepo, emptyStore, { tree: false, history: true });
+  console.log(`history rename-old-path=${hr.findings.some((f) => f.cls === "secret-file" && f.where.startsWith("state.json"))} annotated-tag=${hr.findings.some((f) => f.cls === "seed" && f.where.startsWith("tag "))} merge-message=${hr.findings.some((f) => f.cls === "seed" && f.where.startsWith(`commit ${mergeSha}`))}`);
+
+  console.log("\nSCRUBBER");
+  const unreported = `X${homedir()}/file`;
+  const scrubbed = scrub(unreported);
+  console.log(`non-detector substring: pre-path-hits=${pathHits(unreported).filter((h) => !h.allowedBy).length} changed=${scrubbed.text !== unreported}`);
+  const winFile = join(scratch, "windows.txt");
+  writeFileSync(winFile, "C:[synthetic personal-path redacted]/work\n");
+  const scrubRun = run(["bun", "run", join(import.meta.dir, "scrub-personal-data.ts"), winFile], ROOT);
+  console.log(`forward-slash Windows path scrubber exit=${scrubRun.exit}; ${scrubRun.out.trimEnd().split("\n").at(-1)}`);
+
+  console.log("\nUNTESTED MUTATION");
+  const clone = join(scratch, "mutant-repo");
+  const cloneRun = run(["git", "clone", "-q", "--no-hardlinks", ROOT, clone], ROOT);
+  if (cloneRun.exit !== 0) throw new Error(cloneRun.err);
+  symlinkSync(join(ROOT, "node_modules"), join(clone, "node_modules"), "dir");
+  const mutantGate = join(clone, "tools", "check-no-secrets.ts");
+  const source = readFileSync(mutantGate, "utf8");
+  const openAiLine = '  { name: "OpenAI API key", re: /\\bsk-(?:proj-)?[A-Za-z0-9_-]{40,}/g },\n';
+  if (!source.includes(openAiLine)) throw new Error("OpenAI mutation did not apply");
+  writeFileSync(mutantGate, source.replace(openAiLine, ""));
+  const controls = run(["bun", "run", mutantGate, "--controls-only"], clone);
+  const harness = run(["bun", "run", join(clone, "tools", "break-no-secrets-gate.ts")], clone);
+  put(clone, "synthetic-openai-token.txt", `sk-${"A".repeat(40)}\n`);
+  const leakRun = run(["bun", "run", mutantGate, "--tree-only", "--no-local-store"], clone);
+  console.log(`deleted OpenAI detector controls: ${controls.out.trimEnd().split("\n").at(-1)}`);
+  console.log(`deleted OpenAI detector harness: ${harness.out.trimEnd().split("\n").at(-1)}`);
+  console.log(`deleted OpenAI detector planted token: ${leakRun.out.trimEnd().split("\n").at(-1)}`);
+} finally {
+  rmSync(scratch, { recursive: true, force: true });
+}

+import { mkdtempSync, readFileSync, rmSync, symlinkSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
+import { tmpdir } from "node:os";
+import { join } from "node:path";
+
+const ROOT = join(import.meta.dir, "..");
+const scratch = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), "review056-mutant-"));
+const clone = join(scratch, "repo");
+const env = {
+  ...process.env,
+  GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM: "1",
+  GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL: "/dev/null",
+  GIT_AUTHOR_NAME: "probe",
+  GIT_AUTHOR_EMAIL: "probe@example.invalid",
+  GIT_COMMITTER_NAME: "probe",
+  GIT_COMMITTER_EMAIL: "probe@example.invalid",
+};
+const run = (cmd: string[]) => {
+  const r = Bun.spawnSync(cmd, { cwd: clone, stdout: "pipe", stderr: "pipe", env });
+  return { exit: r.exitCode ?? -1, out: new TextDecoder().decode(r.stdout), err: new TextDecoder().decode(r.stderr) };
+};
+
+try {
+  const c = Bun.spawnSync(["git", "clone", "-q", "--no-hardlinks", ROOT, clone], { stdout: "pipe", stderr: "pipe", env });
+  if (c.exitCode !== 0) throw new Error(new TextDecoder().decode(c.stderr));
+  symlinkSync(join(ROOT, "node_modules"), join(clone, "node_modules"), "dir");
+  const gate = join(clone, "tools", "check-no-secrets.ts");
+  const source = readFileSync(gate, "utf8");
+  const line = '  { name: "OpenAI API key", re: /\\bsk-(?:proj-)?[A-Za-z0-9_-]{40,}/g },\n';
+  writeFileSync(gate, source.replace(line, ""));
+
+  const controls = run(["bun", "run", gate, "--controls-only"]);
+  console.log("controls", controls.exit, controls.out.trimEnd().split("\n").at(-1));
+  const harness = run(["bun", "run", join(clone, "tools", "break-no-secrets-gate.ts")]);
+  console.log("harness", harness.exit);
+  console.log(harness.out.split("\n").filter((l) => l.startsWith("✗") || l.includes("verdict=") || l.includes("baseline: the real tree")).join("\n"));
+  writeFileSync(join(clone, "synthetic-openai-token.txt"), `sk-${"A".repeat(40)}\n`);
+  const leak = run(["bun", "run", gate, "--tree-only", "--no-local-store"]);
+  console.log("leak", leak.exit, leak.out.split("\n").filter((l) => l.startsWith("FAILING") || l.startsWith("  ") || l.startsWith("check-no-secrets:")).slice(-30).join("\n"));
+} finally {
+  rmSync(scratch, { recursive: true, force: true });
+}

+import { randomBytes } from "node:crypto";
+import {
+  mkdirSync, mkdtempSync,
+  readFileSync, rmSync, symlinkSync, writeFileSync,
+} from "node:fs";
+import { homedir, tmpdir } from "node:os";
+import { dirname, join } from "node:path";
+import { encodeSeed } from "ripple-address-codec";
+import {
+  CITED_VECTORS, loadStore, pathHits, scanRepo, scanText,
+} from "./check-no-secrets.ts";
+import { scrub } from "./scrub-personal-data.ts";
+
+const ROOT = join(import.meta.dir, "..");
+const GATE = join(import.meta.dir, "check-no-secrets.ts");
+const scratch = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), "review056-"));
+const gitEnv = {
+  GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM: "1",
+  GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL: "/dev/null",
+  GIT_AUTHOR_NAME: "probe",
+  GIT_AUTHOR_EMAIL: "probe@example.invalid",
+  GIT_COMMITTER_NAME: "probe",
+  GIT_COMMITTER_EMAIL: "probe@example.invalid",
+};
+
+function run(cmd: string[], cwd = ROOT, input?: string, env = gitEnv) {
+  const r = Bun.spawnSync(cmd, { cwd, ...(input === undefined ? {} : { stdin: new TextEncoder().encode(input) }), stdout: "pipe", stderr: "pipe", env: { ...process.env, ...env } });
+  return {
+    exit: r.exitCode ?? -1,
+    out: new TextDecoder().decode(r.stdout),
+    err: new TextDecoder().decode(r.stderr),
+  };
+}
+
+function g(repo: string, ...args: string[]) {
+  const r = run(["git", ...args], repo);
+  if (r.exit !== 0) throw new Error(`git ${args.join(" ")} failed: ${r.err}`);
+  return r.out.trim();
+}
+
+function init(repo: string) {
+  mkdirSync(repo, { recursive: true });
+  g(repo, "init", "-q", "-b", "main");
+}
+
+function put(repo: string, rel: string, body: string) {
+  const p = join(repo, rel);
+  mkdirSync(dirname(p), { recursive: true });
+  writeFileSync(p, body);
+}
+
+function gate(args: string[]) {
+  const r = run(["bun", "run", GATE, ...args], ROOT);
+  const lines = r.out.trimEnd().split("\n");
+  return { exit: r.exit, last: lines.at(-1) ?? "", scanned: lines.find((l) => l.startsWith("scanned:")) ?? "" };
+}
+
+const emptyStore = { files: [], needles: [], secrets: 0, publicSkipped: 0 };
+const scalar64 = "a".repeat(64);
+const scalar63 = "a".repeat(63);
+
+try {
+  console.log("DETECTOR SHAPES");
+  const shapes: [string, string][] = [
+    ["63-digit labelled scalar", `private_key: ${scalar63}`],
+    ["pretty JSON scalar on next line", `{"private_key":\n  "${scalar64}"}`],
+    ["Markdown value before label", `| ${scalar64} | private key |`],
+    ["pretty JSON seed entropy on next line", `{"seed":\n  "${randomBytes(16).toString("hex")}"}`],
+    ["Windows forward-slash home", "C:[synthetic personal-path redacted]/work"],
+    ["AWS secret access key", `aws_secret_access_key=${"A".repeat(40)}`],
+  ];
+  for (const [name, text] of shapes) console.log(`${name}: findings=${scanText(text, emptyStore).filter((h) => !h.allowedBy).length}`);
+
+  const toy = `private_key: ${"0".repeat(63)}7`;
+  const publicSeed = encodeSeed(Buffer.alloc(16, 0x42), "ed25519");
+  const toyHits = scanText(toy, emptyStore);
+  const publicHits = scanText(`production_seed=${publicSeed}`, emptyStore);
+  console.log(`toy scalar: findings=${toyHits.filter((h) => !h.allowedBy).length} allowed=${toyHits.some((h) => h.allowedBy?.includes("toy scalar"))}`);
+  console.log(`repeated-byte production seed: findings=${publicHits.filter((h) => !h.allowedBy).length} allowed=${publicHits.some((h) => h.allowedBy?.includes("public entropy"))}`);
+
+  const storeDir = join(scratch, "malformed-store");
+  mkdirSync(storeDir);
+  const storedSeed = encodeSeed(randomBytes(16), "ed25519");
+  const omittedScalar = randomBytes(32).toString("hex");
+  writeFileSync(join(storeDir, "good.json"), JSON.stringify({ seed: storedSeed }));
+  writeFileSync(join(storeDir, "broken.json"), `{"sk":"${omittedScalar}"`);
+  const malformedStore = loadStore([storeDir], {});
+  console.log(`malformed store: secrets=${malformedStore.secrets} files=${malformedStore.files.length} bare-scalar-findings=${scanText(omittedScalar, malformedStore).filter((h) => !h.allowedBy).length}`);
+
+  console.log("\nENUMERATION");
+  const filterRepo = join(scratch, "filter-repo");
+  init(filterRepo);
+  const filteredSeed = encodeSeed(randomBytes(16), "ed25519");
+  put(filterRepo, ".gitattributes", "payload.txt filter=inject\n");
+  put(filterRepo, "payload.txt", "SAFE\n");
+  put(filterRepo, "cited.txt", CITED_VECTORS.map((v) => `private_key: ${v.value}`).join("\n") + "\n");
+  g(filterRepo, "config", "filter.inject.clean", `sed s/SAFE/${filteredSeed}/g`);
+  const beforeAdd = gate(["--repo", filterRepo, "--tree-only", "--no-local-store"]);
+  g(filterRepo, "add", "-A");
+  g(filterRepo, "commit", "-q", "--no-gpg-sign", "-m", "filtered");
+  const committedFiltered = g(filterRepo, "show", "HEAD:payload.txt").includes(filteredSeed);
+  const afterAdd = gate(["--repo", filterRepo, "--rev", "HEAD", "--no-local-store"]);
+  console.log(`clean filter before git add: ${beforeAdd.last}`);
+  console.log(`clean filter committed secret=${committedFiltered}; after commit: ${afterAdd.last}`);
+
+  const replaceRepo = join(scratch, "replace-repo");
+  init(replaceRepo);
+  const replacedSeed = encodeSeed(randomBytes(16), "secp256k1");
+  put(replaceRepo, "leak.txt", replacedSeed + "\n");
+  g(replaceRepo, "add", "-A");
+  g(replaceRepo, "commit", "-q", "--no-gpg-sign", "-m", "secret object");

+  g(replaceRepo, "replace", oldBlob, cleanBlob);
+  writeFileSync(join(replaceRepo, "leak.txt"), "SAFE\n");
+  g(replaceRepo, "replace", "-d", oldBlob);
+  const original = g(replaceRepo, "cat-file", "blob", oldBlob).includes(replacedSeed);
+  g(replaceRepo, "replace", oldBlob, cleanBlob);
+  const revReplace = gate(["--repo", replaceRepo, "--rev", "HEAD", "--no-local-store"]);
+  const histReplace = gate(["--repo", replaceRepo, "--history-only", "--no-local-store"]);
+  console.log(`replace ref original secret=${original}; rev: ${revReplace.last}; history: ${histReplace.last}`);
+
+  const tabRepo = join(scratch, "tab-repo");
+  init(tabRepo);
+  put(tabRepo, "safe\t/Users/Alice/state.json", "SAFE\n");
+  g(tabRepo, "add", "-A");
+  g(tabRepo, "commit", "-q", "--no-gpg-sign", "-m", "odd path");
+  const rawPathHasSecretShape = g(tabRepo, "ls-tree", "-r", "--name-only", "HEAD").includes("state.json");

+};

+try {
+  const c = Bun.spawnSync(["git", "clone", "-q", "--no-hardlinks", ROOT, clone], { stdout: "pipe", stderr: "pipe", env });
+  if (c.exitCode !== 0) throw new Error(new TextDecoder().decode(c.stderr));
+  symlinkSync(join(ROOT, "node_modules"), join(clone, "node_modules"), "dir");
+  const gate = join(clone, "tools", "check-no-secrets.ts");
+  const source = readFileSync(gate, "utf8");
+  const line = '  { name: "OpenAI API key", re: /\\bsk-(?:proj-)?[A-Za-z0-9_-]{40,}/g },\n';
+  writeFileSync(gate, source.replace(line, ""));
+
+  const controls = run(["bun", "run", gate, "--controls-only"]);
+  console.log("controls", controls.exit, controls.out.trimEnd().split("\n").at(-1));
+  const harness = run(["bun", "run", join(clone, "tools", "break-no-secrets-gate.ts")]);
+  console.log("harness", harness.exit);
+  console.log(harness.out.split("\n").filter((l) => l.startsWith("✗") || l.includes("verdict=") || l.includes("baseline: the real tree")).join("\n"));
+  writeFileSync(join(clone, "synthetic-openai-token.txt"), `sk-${"A".repeat(40)}\n`);
+  const leak = run(["bun", "run", gate, "--tree-only", "--no-local-store"]);
+  console.log("leak", leak.exit, leak.out.split("\n").filter((l) => l.startsWith("FAILING") || l.startsWith("  ") || l.startsWith("check-no-secrets:")).slice(-30).join("\n"));
+} finally {
+  rmSync(scratch, { recursive: true, force: true });

+import { randomBytes } from "node:crypto";
+import {
+  mkdirSync, mkdtempSync,
+  readFileSync, rmSync, symlinkSync, writeFileSync,
+} from "node:fs";
+import { homedir, tmpdir } from "node:os";
+import { dirname, join } from "node:path";
+import { encodeSeed } from "ripple-address-codec";
+import {
+  CITED_VECTORS, loadStore, pathHits, scanRepo, scanText,
+} from "./check-no-secrets.ts";
+import { scrub } from "./scrub-personal-data.ts";
+
+const ROOT = join(import.meta.dir, "..");
+const GATE = join(import.meta.dir, "check-no-secrets.ts");
+const scratch = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), "review056-"));
+const gitEnv = {
+  GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM: "1",
+  GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL: "/dev/null",
+  GIT_AUTHOR_NAME: "probe",
+  GIT_AUTHOR_EMAIL: "probe@example.invalid",
+  GIT_COMMITTER_NAME: "probe",
+  GIT_COMMITTER_EMAIL: "probe@example.invalid",
+};
+
+function run(cmd: string[], cwd = ROOT, input?: string, env = gitEnv) {
+  const r = Bun.spawnSync(cmd, { cwd, ...(input === undefined ? {} : { stdin: new TextEncoder().encode(input) }), stdout: "pipe", stderr: "pipe", env: { ...process.env, ...env } });
+  return {
+    exit: r.exitCode ?? -1,
+    out: new TextDecoder().decode(r.stdout),
+    err: new TextDecoder().decode(r.stderr),
+  };
+}
+
+function g(repo: string, ...args: string[]) {
+  const r = run(["git", ...args], repo);
+  if (r.exit !== 0) throw new Error(`git ${args.join(" ")} failed: ${r.err}`);
+  return r.out.trim();
+}
+
+function init(repo: string) {
+  mkdirSync(repo, { recursive: true });
+  g(repo, "init", "-q", "-b", "main");
+}
+
+function put(repo: string, rel: string, body: string) {
+  const p = join(repo, rel);
+  mkdirSync(dirname(p), { recursive: true });
+  writeFileSync(p, body);
+}
+
+function gate(args: string[]) {
+  const r = run(["bun", "run", GATE, ...args], ROOT);
+  const lines = r.out.trimEnd().split("\n");
+  return { exit: r.exit, last: lines.at(-1) ?? "", scanned: lines.find((l) => l.startsWith("scanned:")) ?? "" };
+}
+
+const emptyStore = { files: [], needles: [], secrets: 0, publicSkipped: 0 };
+const scalar64 = "a".repeat(64);
+const scalar63 = "a".repeat(63);
+
+try {
+  console.log("DETECTOR SHAPES");
+  const shapes: [string, string][] = [
+    ["63-digit labelled scalar", `private_key: ${scalar63}`],
+    ["pretty JSON scalar on next line", `{"private_key":\n  "${scalar64}"}`],
+    ["Markdown value before label", `| ${scalar64} | private key |`],
+    ["pretty JSON seed entropy on next line", `{"seed":\n  "${randomBytes(16).toString("hex")}"}`],
+    ["Windows forward-slash home", "C:[synthetic personal-path redacted]/work"],
+    ["AWS secret access key", `aws_secret_access_key=${"A".repeat(40)}`],
+  ];
+  for (const [name, text] of shapes) console.log(`${name}: findings=${scanText(text, emptyStore).filter((h) => !h.allowedBy).length}`);
+
+  const toy = `private_key: ${"0".repeat(63)}7`;
+  const publicSeed = encodeSeed(Buffer.alloc(16, 0x42), "ed25519");
+  const toyHits = scanText(toy, emptyStore);
+  const publicHits = scanText(`production_seed=${publicSeed}`, emptyStore);
+  console.log(`toy scalar: findings=${toyHits.filter((h) => !h.allowedBy).length} allowed=${toyHits.some((h) => h.allowedBy?.includes("toy scalar"))}`);
+  console.log(`repeated-byte production seed: findings=${publicHits.filter((h) => !h.allowedBy).length} allowed=${publicHits.some((h) => h.allowedBy?.includes("public entropy"))}`);
+
+  const storeDir = join(scratch, "malformed-store");
+  mkdirSync(storeDir);
+  const storedSeed = encodeSeed(randomBytes(16), "ed25519");
+  const omittedScalar = randomBytes(32).toString("hex");
+  writeFileSync(join(storeDir, "good.json"), JSON.stringify({ seed: storedSeed }));
+  writeFileSync(join(storeDir, "broken.json"), `{"sk":"${omittedScalar}"`);
+  const malformedStore = loadStore([storeDir], {});
+  console.log(`malformed store: secrets=${malformedStore.secrets} files=${malformedStore.files.length} bare-scalar-findings=${scanText(omittedScalar, malformedStore).filter((h) => !h.allowedBy).length}`);
+
+  console.log("\nENUMERATION");
+  const filterRepo = join(scratch, "filter-repo");
+  init(filterRepo);
+  const filteredSeed = encodeSeed(randomBytes(16), "ed25519");
+  put(filterRepo, ".gitattributes", "payload.txt filter=inject\n");
+  put(filterRepo, "payload.txt", "SAFE\n");
+  put(filterRepo, "cited.txt", CITED_VECTORS.map((v) => `private_key: ${v.value}`).join("\n") + "\n");
+  g(filterRepo, "config", "filter.inject.clean", `sed s/SAFE/${filteredSeed}/g`);
+  const beforeAdd = gate(["--repo", filterRepo, "--tree-only", "--no-local-store"]);
+  g(filterRepo, "add", "-A");
+  g(filterRepo, "commit", "-q", "--no-gpg-sign", "-m", "filtered");
+  const committedFiltered = g(filterRepo, "show", "HEAD:payload.txt").includes(filteredSeed);
+  const afterAdd = gate(["--repo", filterRepo, "--rev", "HEAD", "--no-local-store"]);
+  console.log(`clean filter before git add: ${beforeAdd.last}`);
+  console.log(`clean filter committed secret=${committedFiltered}; after commit: ${afterAdd.last}`);
+
+  const replaceRepo = join(scratch, "replace-repo");
+  init(replaceRepo);
+  const replacedSeed = encodeSeed(randomBytes(16), "secp256k1");
+  put(replaceRepo, "leak.txt", replacedSeed + "\n");
+  g(replaceRepo, "add", "-A");
+  g(replaceRepo, "commit", "-q", "--no-gpg-sign", "-m", "secret object");
+  const oldBlob = g(replaceRepo, "rev-parse", "HEAD:leak.txt");
+  const cleanBlob = run(["git", "hash-object", "-w", "--stdin"], replaceRepo, "SAFE\n").out.trim();
+  g(replaceRepo, "replace", oldBlob, cleanBlob);
+  writeFileSync(join(replaceRepo, "leak.txt"), "SAFE\n");
+  g(replaceRepo, "replace", "-d", oldBlob);
+  const original = g(replaceRepo, "cat-file", "blob", oldBlob).includes(replacedSeed);
+  g(replaceRepo, "replace", oldBlob, cleanBlob);
+  const revReplace = gate(["--repo", replaceRepo, "--rev", "HEAD", "--no-local-store"]);
+  const histReplace = gate(["--repo", replaceRepo, "--history-only", "--no-local-store"]);
+  console.log(`replace ref original secret=${original}; rev: ${revReplace.last}; history: ${histReplace.last}`);
+
+  const tabRepo = join(scratch, "tab-repo");
+  init(tabRepo);
+  put(tabRepo, "safe\t/Users/Alice/state.json", "SAFE\n");
+  g(tabRepo, "add", "-A");
+  g(tabRepo, "commit", "-q", "--no-gpg-sign", "-m", "odd path");
+  const rawPathHasSecretShape = g(tabRepo, "ls-tree", "-r", "--name-only", "HEAD").includes("state.json");
+  const tabRev = gate(["--repo", tabRepo, "--rev", "HEAD", "--no-local-store"]);
+  console.log(`tab filename contains secret path=${rawPathHasSecretShape}; rev: ${tabRev.last}`);
+
+  const sub = join(scratch, "sub");
+  init(sub);
+  put(sub, "ok.txt", "SAFE\n");
+  g(sub, "add", "-A");
+  g(sub, "commit", "-q", "--no-gpg-sign", "-m", "sub");
+  const superRepo = join(scratch, "super");
+  init(superRepo);
+  g(superRepo, "-c", "protocol.file.allow=always", "submodule", "add", "-q", sub, "vendor/sub");
+  g(superRepo, "commit", "-q", "--no-gpg-sign", "-am", "submodule");
+  const subRev = gate(["--repo", superRepo, "--rev", "HEAD", "--no-local-store"]);
+  console.log(`ordinary submodule rev: ${subRev.last}`);
+
+  const histRepo = join(scratch, "history-repo");
+  init(histRepo);
+  put(histRepo, "state.json", "{}\n");
+  g(histRepo, "add", "-A");
+  g(histRepo, "commit", "-q", "--no-gpg-sign", "-m", "old path");
+  g(histRepo, "mv", "state.json", "safe.txt");
+  g(histRepo, "commit", "-q", "--no-gpg-sign", "-m", "rename");
+  const tagSeed = encodeSeed(randomBytes(16), "ed25519");
+  g(histRepo, "tag", "-a", "probe-tag", "-m", `tag carries ${tagSeed}`);
+  g(histRepo, "checkout", "-q", "-b", "side", "HEAD~1");
+  put(histRepo, "side.txt", "SAFE\n");
+  g(histRepo, "add", "-A");
+  g(histRepo, "commit", "-q", "--no-gpg-sign", "-m", "side");
+  g(histRepo, "checkout", "-q", "main");
+  const mergeSeed = encodeSeed(randomBytes(16), "secp256k1");
+  g(histRepo, "merge", "--no-ff", "--no-gpg-sign", "-m", `merge carries ${mergeSeed}`, "side");
+  const mergeSha = g(histRepo, "rev-parse", "HEAD").slice(0, 10);
+  const hr = scanRepo(histRepo, emptyStore, { tree: false, history: true });
+  console.log(`history rename-old-path=${hr.findings.some((f) => f.cls === "secret-file" && f.where.startsWith("state.json"))} annotated-tag=${hr.findings.some((f) => f.cls === "seed" && f.where.startsWith("tag "))} merge-message=${hr.findings.some((f) => f.cls === "seed" && f.where.startsWith(`commit ${mergeSha}`))}`);
+
+  console.log("\nSCRUBBER");
+  const unreported = `X${homedir()}/file`;
+  const scrubbed = scrub(unreported);
+  console.log(`non-detector substring: pre-path-hits=${pathHits(unreported).filter((h) => !h.allowedBy).length} changed=${scrubbed.text !== unreported}`);
+  const winFile = join(scratch, "windows.txt");
+  writeFileSync(winFile, "C:[synthetic personal-path redacted]/work\n");
+  const scrubRun = run(["bun", "run", join(import.meta.dir, "scrub-personal-data.ts"), winFile], ROOT);
+  console.log(`forward-slash Windows path scrubber exit=${scrubRun.exit}; ${scrubRun.out.trimEnd().split("\n").at(-1)}`);
+
+  console.log("\nUNTESTED MUTATION");
+  const clone = join(scratch, "mutant-repo");
+  const cloneRun = run(["git", "clone", "-q", "--no-hardlinks", ROOT, clone], ROOT);
+  if (cloneRun.exit !== 0) throw new Error(cloneRun.err);
+  symlinkSync(join(ROOT, "node_modules"), join(clone, "node_modules"), "dir");
+  const mutantGate = join(clone, "tools", "check-no-secrets.ts");
+  const source = readFileSync(mutantGate, "utf8");
+  const openAiLine = '  { name: "OpenAI API key", re: /\\bsk-(?:proj-)?[A-Za-z0-9_-]{40,}/g },\n';
+  if (!source.includes(openAiLine)) throw new Error("OpenAI mutation did not apply");
+  writeFileSync(mutantGate, source.replace(openAiLine, ""));
+  const controls = run(["bun", "run", mutantGate, "--controls-only"], clone);
+  const harness = run(["bun", "run", join(clone, "tools", "break-no-secrets-gate.ts")], clone);
+  put(clone, "synthetic-openai-token.txt", `sk-${"A".repeat(40)}\n`);
+  const leakRun = run(["bun", "run", mutantGate, "--tree-only", "--no-local-store"], clone);
+  console.log(`deleted OpenAI detector controls: ${controls.out.trimEnd().split("\n").at(-1)}`);
+  console.log(`deleted OpenAI detector harness: ${harness.out.trimEnd().split("\n").at(-1)}`);
+  console.log(`deleted OpenAI detector planted token: ${leakRun.out.trimEnd().split("\n").at(-1)}`);
+} finally {
+  rmSync(scratch, { recursive: true, force: true });
+}

/**
 * wconf-keygen.ts — the ruled ElGamal derivation, for TypeScript callers.
 *
 * # Why this file exists
 *
 * `REVIEW-012-RULING-keygen-2026-08-18.md` §2 rules how a holder's ElGamal
 * secret is derived from their XRPL master seed, and
 * `docs/reviews/REVIEW-012c` adjudicated it. The construction lives in that
 * ruling and **only** there; this is an *implementation* of its prose, and
 * where the two disagree **the ruling wins**.
 *
 * Before 2026-08-25 there were two byte-identical copies of it in `tools/` —
 * one in `spike-step6-2026-08-20/devnet-derived-key.ts`, one in
 * `spike-step7-2026-08-24/devnet-convert-back.ts` — and architecture §6 step 7
 * was about to add a third. `REVIEW-005c`'s lesson is that a second copy of a
 * construction is a divergence waiting to happen; a third is that lesson
 * ignored twice. This is the single copy those callers now import.
 *
 * # ⚠ What the independence that matters actually is
 *
 * The cross-check that makes the Devnet drivers evidence is **TypeScript
 * against Rust**, mediated by a validated ledger entry: `signer/src/keygen.rs`
 * derives the key the account registers, and a driver derives it again here
 * and compares. Collapsing three identical TypeScript copies into one does not
 * weaken that, because they never differed. What it removes is three places
 * for a future edit to land in two of.
 *
 * ⚠ `tools/spike-keygen-2026-08-18/t-keygen-construction.ts` stays the KAT and
 * stays independent — it runs the construction through **two** library paths
 * (raw HMAC and @noble's `hkdf`) precisely so the vectors are reproducible from
 * the prose rather than from one code path. **A KAT that imported the thing it
 * tests would be worthless**, so it does not import this and this does not
 * import it.
 *
 * # The self-check, and why it is not decoration
 *
 * Like `wconf-randomness.ts`, this **reproduces the ruling's published vectors
 * before it will derive anything** — including the `PRK` and `OKM`
 * intermediates, so a divergence is located rather than merely detected. A
 * caller that gets a key from this module has already been told the module
 * agrees with §6 of the ruling.
 *
 * # ⚠ The reduction is TOTAL and has NO abort path
 *
 * Unlike the sigma-nonce and `r`/`ρ` rulings, which both say *abort on zero,
 * never retry*. That is correct there, where the input is fresh entropy and an
 * abort costs one transaction. **Keygen's input is a fixed seed**, so an abort
 * would be permanent — that holder could never participate. `(OKM mod (n−1)) +
 * 1` cannot yield zero and cannot yield `n`. Do not "fix" it back.
 *
 * # ⚠ What deriving buys, and what it does not
 *
 * A derived key is reconstructible from the master seed **under this
 * derivation**, which is the only thing deriving buys. It is not "recoverable"
 * in any wider sense: XLS-0096 has no rotation and no recovery (`CLAIMS.md`
 * P-10), and the derivation is **not a published standard** (issue #10), so a
 * holder who has their seed and not this repository cannot reconstruct their
 * key. Those are two sentences about two layers and merging them is
 * `CLAIMS.md` E-125 and E-126.
 *
 *   bun tools/wconf-keygen.ts        # runs the self-check and prints the result
 */

+function run(cmd: string[], cwd = ROOT, input?: string, env = gitEnv) {
+  const r = Bun.spawnSync(cmd, { cwd, ...(input === undefined ? {} : { stdin: new TextEncoder().encode(input) }), stdout: "pipe", stderr: "pipe", env: { ...process.env, ...env } });

+    out: new TextDecoder().decode(r.stdout),
+    err: new TextDecoder().decode(r.stderr),
+  };

+function g(repo: string, ...args: string[]) {
+  const r = run(["git", ...args], repo);
+  if (r.exit !== 0) throw new Error(`git ${args.join(" ")} failed: ${r.err}`);
+  return r.out.trim();
+}

+function init(repo: string) {
+  mkdirSync(repo, { recursive: true });

+  const tagSeed = encodeSeed(randomBytes(16), "ed25519");
+  g(histRepo, "tag", "-a", "probe-tag", "-m", `tag carries ${tagSeed}`);
+  g(histRepo, "checkout", "-q", "-b", "side", "HEAD~1");
+  put(histRepo, "side.txt", "SAFE\n");
+  g(histRepo, "add", "-A");
+  g(histRepo, "commit", "-q", "--no-gpg-sign", "-m", "side");
+  g(histRepo, "checkout", "-q", "main");
+  const mergeSeed = encodeSeed(randomBytes(16), "secp256k1");
+  g(histRepo, "merge", "--no-ff", "--no-gpg-sign", "-m", `merge carries ${mergeSeed}`, "side");
+  const mergeSha = g(histRepo, "rev-parse", "HEAD").slice(0, 10);
+  const hr = scanRepo(histRepo, emptyStore, { tree: false, history: true });
+  console.log(`history rename-old-path=${hr.findings.some((f) => f.cls === "secret-file" && f.where.startsWith("state.json"))} annotated-tag=${hr.findings.some((f) => f.cls === "seed" && f.where.startsWith("tag "))} merge-message=${hr.findings.some((f) => f.cls === "seed" && f.where.startsWith(`commit ${mergeSha}`))}`);
+
+  console.log("\nSCRUBBER");
+  const unreported = `X${homedir()}/file`;
+  const scrubbed = scrub(unreported);
+  console.log(`non-detector substring: pre-path-hits=${pathHits(unreported).filter((h) => !h.allowedBy).length} changed=${scrubbed.text !== unreported}`);
+  const winFile = join(scratch, "windows.txt");
+  writeFileSync(winFile, "C:[synthetic personal-path redacted]/work\n");
+  const scrubRun = run(["bun", "run", join(import.meta.dir, "scrub-personal-data.ts"), winFile], ROOT);
+  console.log(`forward-slash Windows path scrubber exit=${scrubRun.exit}; ${scrubRun.out.trimEnd().split("\n").at(-1)}`);
+
+  console.log("\nUNTESTED MUTATION");
+  const clone = join(scratch, "mutant-repo");
+  const cloneRun = run(["git", "clone", "-q", "--no-hardlinks", ROOT, clone], ROOT);
+  if (cloneRun.exit !== 0) throw new Error(cloneRun.err);
+  symlinkSync(join(ROOT, "node_modules"), join(clone, "node_modules"), "dir");
+  const mutantGate = join(clone, "tools", "check-no-secrets.ts");
+  const source = readFileSync(mutantGate, "utf8");
+  const openAiLine = '  { name: "OpenAI API key", re: /\\bsk-(?:proj-)?[A-Za-z0-9_-]{40,}/g },\n';
+  if (!source.includes(openAiLine)) throw new Error("OpenAI mutation did not apply");
+  writeFileSync(mutantGate, source.replace(openAiLine, ""));
+  const controls = run(["bun", "run", mutantGate, "--controls-only"], clone);
+  const harness = run(["bun", "run", join(clone, "tools", "break-no-secrets-gate.ts")], clone);
+  put(clone, "synthetic-openai-token.txt", `sk-${"A".repeat(40)}\n`);
+  const leakRun = run(["bun", "run", mutantGate, "--tree-only", "--no-local-store"], clone);
+  console.log(`deleted OpenAI detector controls: ${controls.out.trimEnd().split("\n").at(-1)}`);
+  console.log(`deleted OpenAI detector harness: ${harness.out.trimEnd().split("\n").at(-1)}`);
+  console.log(`deleted OpenAI detector planted token: ${leakRun.out.trimEnd().split("\n").at(-1)}`);
+} finally {
+  rmSync(scratch, { recursive: true, force: true });
+}

+import { mkdirSync, mkdtempSync, readFileSync, rmSync, symlinkSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
+import { tmpdir } from "node:os";
+import { join } from "node:path";
+
+const ROOT = join(import.meta.dir, "..");
+const scratch = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), "review056-mutant-"));
+const clone = join(scratch, "repo");
+const env = {
+  ...process.env,
+  GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM: "1",
+  GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL: "/dev/null",
+  GIT_AUTHOR_NAME: "probe",
+  GIT_AUTHOR_EMAIL: "probe@example.invalid",
+  GIT_COMMITTER_NAME: "probe",
+  GIT_COMMITTER_EMAIL: "probe@example.invalid",
+};
+const run = (cmd: string[]) => {
+  const r = Bun.spawnSync(cmd, { cwd: clone, stdout: "pipe", stderr: "pipe", env });
+  return { exit: r.exitCode ?? -1, out: new TextDecoder().decode(r.stdout), err: new TextDecoder().decode(r.stderr) };
+};
+
+try {
+  const c = Bun.spawnSync(["git", "clone", "-q", "--no-hardlinks", ROOT, clone], { stdout: "pipe", stderr: "pipe", env });
+  if (c.exitCode !== 0) throw new Error(new TextDecoder().decode(c.stderr));
+  mkdirSync(join(clone, "node_modules"));
+  for (const pkg of ["@noble", "ripple-address-codec", "ripple-keypairs"])
+    symlinkSync(join(ROOT, "node_modules", pkg), join(clone, "node_modules", pkg), "dir");
+  const gate = join(clone, "tools", "check-no-secrets.ts");
+  const source = readFileSync(gate, "utf8");
+  const line = '  { name: "OpenAI API key", re: /\\bsk-(?:proj-)?[A-Za-z0-9_-]{40,}/g },\n';
+  writeFileSync(gate, source.replace(line, ""));
+
+  const controls = run(["bun", "run", gate, "--controls-only"]);
+  console.log("controls", controls.exit, controls.out.trimEnd().split("\n").at(-1));
+  const harness = run(["bun", "run", join(clone, "tools", "break-no-secrets-gate.ts")]);
+  console.log("harness", harness.exit);
+  console.log(harness.out.split("\n").filter((l) => l.startsWith("✗") || l.includes("verdict=") || l.includes("baseline: the real tree")).join("\n"));
+  writeFileSync(join(clone, "synthetic-openai-token.txt"), `sk-${"A".repeat(40)}\n`);
+  const leak = run(["bun", "run", gate, "--tree-only", "--no-local-store"]);
+  console.log("leak", leak.exit, leak.out.split("\n").filter((l) => l.startsWith("FAILING") || l.startsWith("  ") || l.startsWith("check-no-secrets:")).slice(-30).join("\n"));
+} finally {
+  rmSync(scratch, { recursive: true, force: true });
+}

+import { randomBytes } from "node:crypto";
+import {
+  mkdirSync, mkdtempSync,
+  readFileSync, rmSync, symlinkSync, writeFileSync,
+} from "node:fs";
+import { homedir, tmpdir } from "node:os";
+import { dirname, join } from "node:path";
+import { encodeSeed } from "ripple-address-codec";
+import {
+  CITED_VECTORS, loadStore, pathHits, scanRepo, scanText,
+} from "./check-no-secrets.ts";
+import { scrub } from "./scrub-personal-data.ts";
+
+const ROOT = join(import.meta.dir, "..");
+const GATE = join(import.meta.dir, "check-no-secrets.ts");
+const scratch = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), "review056-"));
+const gitEnv = {
+  GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM: "1",
+  GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL: "/dev/null",
+  GIT_AUTHOR_NAME: "probe",
+  GIT_AUTHOR_EMAIL: "probe@example.invalid",
+  GIT_COMMITTER_NAME: "probe",
+  GIT_COMMITTER_EMAIL: "probe@example.invalid",
+};
+
+function run(cmd: string[], cwd = ROOT, input?: string, env = gitEnv) {
+  const r = Bun.spawnSync(cmd, { cwd, ...(input === undefined ? {} : { stdin: new TextEncoder().encode(input) }), stdout: "pipe", stderr: "pipe", env: { ...process.env, ...env } });
+  return {
+    exit: r.exitCode ?? -1,
+    out: new TextDecoder().decode(r.stdout),
+    err: new TextDecoder().decode(r.stderr),
+  };
+}
+
+function g(repo: string, ...args: string[]) {
+  const r = run(["git", ...args], repo);
+  if (r.exit !== 0) throw new Error(`git ${args.join(" ")} failed: ${r.err}`);
+  return r.out.trim();
+}
+
+function init(repo: string) {
+  mkdirSync(repo, { recursive: true });
+  g(repo, "init", "-q", "-b", "main");
+}
+
+function put(repo: string, rel: string, body: string) {
+  const p = join(repo, rel);
+  mkdirSync(dirname(p), { recursive: true });
+  writeFileSync(p, body);
+}
+
+function gate(args: string[]) {
+  const r = run(["bun", "run", GATE, ...args], ROOT);
+  const lines = r.out.trimEnd().split("\n");
+  return { exit: r.exit, last: lines.at(-1) ?? "", scanned: lines.find((l) => l.startsWith("scanned:")) ?? "" };
+}
+
+const emptyStore = { files: [], needles: [], secrets: 0, publicSkipped: 0 };
+const scalar64 = "a".repeat(64);
+const scalar63 = "a".repeat(63);
+
+try {
+  console.log("DETECTOR SHAPES");
+  const shapes: [string, string][] = [
+    ["63-digit labelled scalar", `private_key: ${scalar63}`],
+    ["pretty JSON scalar on next line", `{"private_key":\n  "${scalar64}"}`],
+    ["Markdown value before label", `| ${scalar64} | private key |`],
+    ["pretty JSON seed entropy on next line", `{"seed":\n  "${randomBytes(16).toString("hex")}"}`],
+    ["Windows forward-slash home", "C:[synthetic personal-path redacted]/work"],
+    ["AWS secret access key", `aws_secret_access_key=${"A".repeat(40)}`],
+  ];
+  for (const [name, text] of shapes) console.log(`${name}: findings=${scanText(text, emptyStore).filter((h) => !h.allowedBy).length}`);
+
+  const toy = `private_key: ${"0".repeat(63)}7`;
+  const publicSeed = encodeSeed(Buffer.alloc(16, 0x42), "ed25519");
+  const toyHits = scanText(toy, emptyStore);
+  const publicHits = scanText(`production_seed=${publicSeed}`, emptyStore);
+  console.log(`toy scalar: findings=${toyHits.filter((h) => !h.allowedBy).length} allowed=${toyHits.some((h) => h.allowedBy?.includes("toy scalar"))}`);
+  console.log(`repeated-byte production seed: findings=${publicHits.filter((h) => !h.allowedBy).length} allowed=${publicHits.some((h) => h.allowedBy?.includes("public entropy"))}`);
+
+  const storeDir = join(scratch, "malformed-store");
+  mkdirSync(storeDir);
+  const storedSeed = encodeSeed(randomBytes(16), "ed25519");
+  const omittedScalar = randomBytes(32).toString("hex");
+  writeFileSync(join(storeDir, "good.json"), JSON.stringify({ seed: storedSeed }));
+  writeFileSync(join(storeDir, "broken.json"), `{"sk":"${omittedScalar}"`);
+  const malformedStore = loadStore([storeDir], {});
+  console.log(`malformed store: secrets=${malformedStore.secrets} files=${malformedStore.files.length} bare-scalar-findings=${scanText(omittedScalar, malformedStore).filter((h) => !h.allowedBy).length}`);
+
+  console.log("\nENUMERATION");
+  const filterRepo = join(scratch, "filter-repo");
+  init(filterRepo);
+  const filteredSeed = encodeSeed(randomBytes(16), "ed25519");
+  put(filterRepo, ".gitattributes", "payload.txt filter=inject\n");
+  put(filterRepo, "payload.txt", "SAFE\n");
+  put(filterRepo, "cited.txt", CITED_VECTORS.map((v) => `private_key: ${v.value}`).join("\n") + "\n");
+  g(filterRepo, "config", "filter.inject.clean", `sed s/SAFE/${filteredSeed}/g`);
+  const beforeAdd = gate(["--repo", filterRepo, "--tree-only", "--no-local-store"]);
+  g(filterRepo, "add", "-A");
+  g(filterRepo, "commit", "-q", "--no-gpg-sign", "-m", "filtered");
+  const committedFiltered = g(filterRepo, "show", "HEAD:payload.txt").includes(filteredSeed);
+  const afterAdd = gate(["--repo", filterRepo, "--rev", "HEAD", "--no-local-store"]);
+  console.log(`clean filter before git add: ${beforeAdd.last}`);
+  console.log(`clean filter committed secret=${committedFiltered}; after commit: ${afterAdd.last}`);
+
+  const replaceRepo = join(scratch, "replace-repo");
+  init(replaceRepo);
+  const replacedSeed = encodeSeed(randomBytes(16), "secp256k1");
+  put(replaceRepo, "leak.txt", replacedSeed + "\n");
+  g(replaceRepo, "add", "-A");
+  g(replaceRepo, "commit", "-q", "--no-gpg-sign", "-m", "secret object");
+  const oldBlob = g(replaceRepo, "rev-parse", "HEAD:leak.txt");
+  const cleanBlob = run(["git", "hash-object", "-w", "--stdin"], replaceRepo, "SAFE\n").out.trim();
+  g(replaceRepo, "replace", oldBlob, cleanBlob);
+  writeFileSync(join(replaceRepo, "leak.txt"), "SAFE\n");
+  g(replaceRepo, "replace", "-d", oldBlob);
+  const original = g(replaceRepo, "cat-file", "blob", oldBlob).includes(replacedSeed);
+  g(replaceRepo, "replace", oldBlob, cleanBlob);
+  const revReplace = gate(["--repo", replaceRepo, "--rev", "HEAD", "--no-local-store"]);
+  const histReplace = gate(["--repo", replaceRepo, "--history-only", "--no-local-store"]);
+  console.log(`replace ref original secret=${original}; rev: ${revReplace.last}; history: ${histReplace.last}`);
+
+  const tabRepo = join(scratch, "tab-repo");
+  init(tabRepo);
+  put(tabRepo, "safe\t/Users/Alice/state.json", "SAFE\n");
+  g(tabRepo, "add", "-A");
+  g(tabRepo, "commit", "-q", "--no-gpg-sign", "-m", "odd path");
+  const rawPathHasSecretShape = g(tabRepo, "ls-tree", "-r", "--name-only", "HEAD").includes("state.json");
+  const tabRev = gate(["--repo", tabRepo, "--rev", "HEAD", "--no-local-store"]);
+  console.log(`tab filename contains secret path=${rawPathHasSecretShape}; rev: ${tabRev.last}`);
+
+  const sub = join(scratch, "sub");
+  init(sub);
+  put(sub, "ok.txt", "SAFE\n");
+  g(sub, "add", "-A");
+  g(sub, "commit", "-q", "--no-gpg-sign", "-m", "sub");
+  const superRepo = join(scratch, "super");
+  init(superRepo);
+  g(superRepo, "-c", "protocol.file.allow=always", "submodule", "add", "-q", sub, "vendor/sub");
+  g(superRepo, "commit", "-q", "--no-gpg-sign", "-am", "submodule");
+  const subRev = gate(["--repo", superRepo, "--rev", "HEAD", "--no-local-store"]);
+  console.log(`ordinary submodule rev: ${subRev.last}`);
+
+  const histRepo = join(scratch, "history-repo");
+  init(histRepo);
+  put(histRepo, "state.json", "{}\n");
+  g(histRepo, "add", "-A");
+  g(histRepo, "commit", "-q", "--no-gpg-sign", "-m", "old path");
+  g(histRepo, "mv", "state.json", "safe.txt");
+  g(histRepo, "commit", "-q", "--no-gpg-sign", "-m", "rename");
+  const tagSeed = encodeSeed(randomBytes(16), "ed25519");
+  g(histRepo, "tag", "-a", "probe-tag", "-m", `tag carries ${tagSeed}`);
+  g(histRepo, "checkout", "-q", "-b", "side", "HEAD~1");
+  put(histRepo, "side.txt", "SAFE\n");
+  g(histRepo, "add", "-A");
+  g(histRepo, "commit", "-q", "--no-gpg-sign", "-m", "side");
+  g(histRepo, "checkout", "-q", "main");
+  const mergeSeed = encodeSeed(randomBytes(16), "secp256k1");
+  g(histRepo, "merge", "--no-ff", "--no-gpg-sign", "-m", `merge carries ${mergeSeed}`, "side");
+  const mergeSha = g(histRepo, "rev-parse", "HEAD").slice(0, 10);
+  const hr = scanRepo(histRepo, emptyStore, { tree: false, history: true });
+  console.log(`history rename-old-path=${hr.findings.some((f) => f.cls === "secret-file" && f.where.startsWith("state.json"))} annotated-tag=${hr.findings.some((f) => f.cls === "seed" && f.where.startsWith("tag "))} merge-message=${hr.findings.some((f) => f.cls === "seed" && f.where.startsWith(`commit ${mergeSha}`))}`);
+
+  console.log("\nSCRUBBER");
+  const unreported = `X${homedir()}/file`;
+  const scrubbed = scrub(unreported);
+  console.log(`non-detector substring: pre-path-hits=${pathHits(unreported).filter((h) => !h.allowedBy).length} changed=${scrubbed.text !== unreported}`);
+  const winFile = join(scratch, "windows.txt");
+  writeFileSync(winFile, "C:[synthetic personal-path redacted]/work\n");

+
+function run(cmd: string[], cwd = ROOT, input?: string, env = gitEnv) {
+  const r = Bun.spawnSync(cmd, { cwd, ...(input === undefined ? {} : { stdin: new TextEncoder().encode(input) }), stdout: "pipe", stderr: "pipe", env: { ...process.env, ...env } });
+  return {
+    exit: r.exitCode ?? -1,
+    out: new TextDecoder().decode(r.stdout),
+    err: new TextDecoder().decode(r.stderr),
+  };
+}
+
+function g(repo: string, ...args: string[]) {
+  const r = run(["git", ...args], repo);
+  if (r.exit !== 0) throw new Error(`git ${args.join(" ")} failed: ${r.err}`);
+  return r.out.trim();
+}
+
+function init(repo: string) {
+  mkdirSync(repo, { recursive: true });
+  g(repo, "init", "-q", "-b", "main");
+}
+
+function put(repo: string, rel: string, body: string) {
+  const p = join(repo, rel);
+  mkdirSync(dirname(p), { recursive: true });
+  writeFileSync(p, body);
+}
+
+function gate(args: string[]) {
+  const r = run(["bun", "run", GATE, ...args], ROOT);
+  const lines = r.out.trimEnd().split("\n");
+  return { exit: r.exit, last: lines.at(-1) ?? "", scanned: lines.find((l) => l.startsWith("scanned:")) ?? "" };
+}
+
+const emptyStore = { files: [], needles: [], secrets: 0, publicSkipped: 0 };
+const scalar64 = "a".repeat(64);
+const scalar63 = "a".repeat(63);
+
+try {
+  console.log("DETECTOR SHAPES");
+  const shapes: [string, string][] = [
+    ["63-digit labelled scalar", `private_key: ${scalar63}`],
+    ["pretty JSON scalar on next line", `{"private_key":\n  "${scalar64}"}`],
+    ["Markdown value before label", `| ${scalar64} | private key |`],
+    ["pretty JSON seed entropy on next line", `{"seed":\n  "${randomBytes(16).toString("hex")}"}`],
+    ["Windows forward-slash home", "C:[synthetic personal-path redacted]/work"],
+    ["AWS secret access key", `aws_secret_access_key=${"A".repeat(40)}`],
+  ];
+  for (const [name, text] of shapes) console.log(`${name}: findings=${scanText(text, emptyStore).filter((h) => !h.allowedBy).length}`);
+
+  const toy = `private_key: ${"0".repeat(63)}7`;
+  const publicSeed = encodeSeed(Buffer.alloc(16, 0x42), "ed25519");
+  const toyHits = scanText(toy, emptyStore);
+  const publicHits = scanText(`production_seed=${publicSeed}`, emptyStore);
+  console.log(`toy scalar: findings=${toyHits.filter((h) => !h.allowedBy).length} allowed=${toyHits.some((h) => h.allowedBy?.includes("toy scalar"))}`);
+  console.log(`repeated-byte production seed: findings=${publicHits.filter((h) => !h.allowedBy).length} allowed=${publicHits.some((h) => h.allowedBy?.includes("public entropy"))}`);
+
+  const storeDir = join(scratch, "malformed-store");
+  mkdirSync(storeDir);
+  const storedSeed = encodeSeed(randomBytes(16), "ed25519");
+  const omittedScalar = randomBytes(32).toString("hex");
+  writeFileSync(join(storeDir, "good.json"), JSON.stringify({ seed: storedSeed }));
+  writeFileSync(join(storeDir, "broken.json"), `{"sk":"${omittedScalar}"`);
+  const malformedStore = loadStore([storeDir], {});
+  console.log(`malformed store: secrets=${malformedStore.secrets} files=${malformedStore.files.length} bare-scalar-findings=${scanText(omittedScalar, malformedStore).filter((h) => !h.allowedBy).length}`);
+
+  console.log("\nENUMERATION");
+  const filterRepo = join(scratch, "filter-repo");
+  init(filterRepo);
+  const filteredSeed = encodeSeed(randomBytes(16), "ed25519");
+  put(filterRepo, ".gitattributes", "payload.txt filter=inject\n");
+  put(filterRepo, "payload.txt", "SAFE\n");
+  put(filterRepo, "cited.txt", CITED_VECTORS.map((v) => `private_key: ${v.value}`).join("\n") + "\n");
+  g(filterRepo, "config", "filter.inject.clean", `sed s/SAFE/${filteredSeed}/g`);
+  const beforeAdd = gate(["--repo", filterRepo, "--tree-only", "--no-local-store"]);
+  g(filterRepo, "add", "-A");
+  g(filterRepo, "commit", "-q", "--no-gpg-sign", "-m", "filtered");
+  const committedFiltered = g(filterRepo, "show", "HEAD:payload.txt").includes(filteredSeed);
+  const afterAdd = gate(["--repo", filterRepo, "--rev", "HEAD", "--no-local-store"]);
+  console.log(`clean filter before git add: ${beforeAdd.last}`);
+  console.log(`clean filter committed secret=${committedFiltered}; after commit: ${afterAdd.last}`);
+
+  const replaceRepo = join(scratch, "replace-repo");
+  init(replaceRepo);
+  const replacedSeed = encodeSeed(randomBytes(16), "secp256k1");
+  put(replaceRepo, "leak.txt", replacedSeed + "\n");
+  g(replaceRepo, "add", "-A");
+  g(replaceRepo, "commit", "-q", "--no-gpg-sign", "-m", "secret object");
+  const oldBlob = g(replaceRepo, "rev-parse", "HEAD:leak.txt");
+  const cleanBlob = run(["git", "hash-object", "-w", "--stdin"], replaceRepo, "SAFE\n").out.trim();
+  g(replaceRepo, "replace", oldBlob, cleanBlob);
+  writeFileSync(join(replaceRepo, "leak.txt"), "SAFE\n");
+  g(replaceRepo, "replace", "-d", oldBlob);
+  const original = g(replaceRepo, "cat-file", "blob", oldBlob).includes(replacedSeed);
+  g(replaceRepo, "replace", oldBlob, cleanBlob);
+  const revReplace = gate(["--repo", replaceRepo, "--rev", "HEAD", "--no-local-store"]);
+  const histReplace = gate(["--repo", replaceRepo, "--history-only", "--no-local-store"]);
+  console.log(`replace ref original secret=${original}; rev: ${revReplace.last}; history: ${histReplace.last}`);
+
+  const tabRepo = join(scratch, "tab-repo");
+  init(tabRepo);
+  put(tabRepo, "safe\t/Users/Alice/state.json", "SAFE\n");
+  g(tabRepo, "add", "-A");
+  g(tabRepo, "commit", "-q", "--no-gpg-sign", "-m", "odd path");
+  const rawPathHasSecretShape = g(tabRepo, "ls-tree", "-r", "--name-only", "HEAD").includes("state.json");
+  const tabRev = gate(["--repo", tabRepo, "--rev", "HEAD", "--no-local-store"]);
+  console.log(`tab filename contains secret path=${rawPathHasSecretShape}; rev: ${tabRev.last}`);
+
+  const sub = join(scratch, "sub");
+  init(sub);
+  put(sub, "ok.txt", "SAFE\n");
+  g(sub, "add", "-A");
+  g(sub, "commit", "-q", "--no-gpg-sign", "-m", "sub");
+  const superRepo = join(scratch, "super");
+  init(superRepo);
+  g(superRepo, "-c", "protocol.file.allow=always", "submodule", "add", "-q", sub, "vendor/sub");
+  g(superRepo, "commit", "-q", "--no-gpg-sign", "-am", "submodule");
+  const subRev = gate(["--repo", superRepo, "--rev", "HEAD", "--no-local-store"]);
+  console.log(`ordinary submodule rev: ${subRev.last}`);
+
+  const histRepo = join(scratch, "history-repo");
+  init(histRepo);
+  put(histRepo, "state.json", "{}\n");
+  g(histRepo, "add", "-A");
+  g(histRepo, "commit", "-q", "--no-gpg-sign", "-m", "old path");
+  g(histRepo, "mv", "state.json", "safe.txt");
+  g(histRepo, "commit", "-q", "--no-gpg-sign", "-m", "rename");
+  const tagSeed = encodeSeed(randomBytes(16), "ed25519");
+  g(histRepo, "tag", "-a", "probe-tag", "-m", `tag carries ${tagSeed}`);
+  g(histRepo, "checkout", "-q", "-b", "side", "HEAD~1");
+  put(histRepo, "side.txt", "SAFE\n");
+  g(histRepo, "add", "-A");
+  g(histRepo, "commit", "-q", "--no-gpg-sign", "-m", "side");
+  g(histRepo, "checkout", "-q", "main");
+  const mergeSeed = encodeSeed(randomBytes(16), "secp256k1");
+  g(histRepo, "merge", "--no-ff", "--no-gpg-sign", "-m", `merge carries ${mergeSeed}`, "side");
+  const mergeSha = g(histRepo, "rev-parse", "HEAD").slice(0, 10);
+  const hr = scanRepo(histRepo, emptyStore, { tree: false, history: true });
+  console.log(`history rename-old-path=${hr.findings.some((f) => f.cls === "secret-file" && f.where.startsWith("state.json"))} annotated-tag=${hr.findings.some((f) => f.cls === "seed" && f.where.startsWith("tag "))} merge-message=${hr.findings.some((f) => f.cls === "seed" && f.where.startsWith(`commit ${mergeSha}`))}`);
+
+  console.log("\nSCRUBBER");
+  const unreported = `X${homedir()}/file`;
+  const scrubbed = scrub(unreported);
+  console.log(`non-detector substring: pre-path-hits=${pathHits(unreported).filter((h) => !h.allowedBy).length} changed=${scrubbed.text !== unreported}`);
+  const winFile = join(scratch, "windows.txt");
+  writeFileSync(winFile, "C:[synthetic personal-path redacted]/work\n");
+  const scrubRun = run(["bun", "run", join(import.meta.dir, "scrub-personal-data.ts"), winFile], ROOT);
+  console.log(`forward-slash Windows path scrubber exit=${scrubRun.exit}; ${scrubRun.out.trimEnd().split("\n").at(-1)}`);
+
+  console.log("\nUNTESTED MUTATION");
+  const clone = join(scratch, "mutant-repo");
+  const cloneRun = run(["git", "clone", "-q", "--no-hardlinks", ROOT, clone], ROOT);
+  if (cloneRun.exit !== 0) throw new Error(cloneRun.err);
+  mkdirSync(join(clone, "node_modules"));
+  for (const pkg of ["@noble", "ripple-address-codec", "ripple-keypairs"])
+    symlinkSync(join(ROOT, "node_modules", pkg), join(clone, "node_modules", pkg), "dir");
+  const mutantGate = join(clone, "tools", "check-no-secrets.ts");
+  const source = readFileSync(mutantGate, "utf8");
+  const openAiLine = '  { name: "OpenAI API key", re: /\\bsk-(?:proj-)?[A-Za-z0-9_-]{40,}/g },\n';
+  if (!source.includes(openAiLine)) throw new Error("OpenAI mutation did not apply");
+  writeFileSync(mutantGate, source.replace(openAiLine, ""));
+  const controls = run(["bun", "run", mutantGate, "--controls-only"], clone);
+  const harness = run(["bun", "run", join(clone, "tools", "break-no-secrets-gate.ts")], clone);
+  put(clone, "synthetic-openai-token.txt", `sk-${"A".repeat(40)}\n`);
+  const leakRun = run(["bun", "run", mutantGate, "--tree-only", "--no-local-store"], clone);
+  console.log(`deleted OpenAI detector controls: ${controls.out.trimEnd().split("\n").at(-1)}`);
+  console.log(`deleted OpenAI detector harness: ${harness.out.trimEnd().split("\n").at(-1)}`);
+  console.log(`deleted OpenAI detector planted token: ${leakRun.out.trimEnd().split("\n").at(-1)}`);
+} finally {
+  rmSync(scratch, { recursive: true, force: true });
+}

+import { mkdirSync, mkdtempSync, readFileSync, rmSync, symlinkSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
+import { tmpdir } from "node:os";
+import { join } from "node:path";
+
+const ROOT = join(import.meta.dir, "..");
+const scratch = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), "review056-mutant-"));
+const clone = join(scratch, "repo");
+const env = {
+  ...process.env,
+  GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM: "1",
+  GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL: "/dev/null",
+  GIT_AUTHOR_NAME: "probe",
+  GIT_AUTHOR_EMAIL: "probe@example.invalid",
+  GIT_COMMITTER_NAME: "probe",
+  GIT_COMMITTER_EMAIL: "probe@example.invalid",
+};
+const run = (cmd: string[]) => {
+  const r = Bun.spawnSync(cmd, { cwd: clone, stdout: "pipe", stderr: "pipe", env });
+  return { exit: r.exitCode ?? -1, out: new TextDecoder().decode(r.stdout), err: new TextDecoder().decode(r.stderr) };
+};
+
+try {
+  const c = Bun.spawnSync(["git", "clone", "-q", "--no-hardlinks", ROOT, clone], { stdout: "pipe", stderr: "pipe", env });
+  if (c.exitCode !== 0) throw new Error(new TextDecoder().decode(c.stderr));
+  mkdirSync(join(clone, "node_modules"));
+  for (const pkg of ["@noble", "ripple-address-codec", "ripple-keypairs"])
+    symlinkSync(join(ROOT, "node_modules", pkg), join(clone, "node_modules", pkg), "dir");
+  const gate = join(clone, "tools", "check-no-secrets.ts");
+  const source = readFileSync(gate, "utf8");
+  const line = '  { name: "OpenAI API key", re: /\\bsk-(?:proj-)?[A-Za-z0-9_-]{40,}/g },\n';
+  writeFileSync(gate, source.replace(line, ""));
+
+  const controls = run(["bun", "run", gate, "--controls-only"]);
+  console.log("controls", controls.exit, controls.out.trimEnd().split("\n").at(-1));
+  const harness = run(["bun", "run", join(clone, "tools", "break-no-secrets-gate.ts")]);
+  console.log("harness", harness.exit);
+  console.log(harness.out.split("\n").filter((l) => l.startsWith("✗") || l.includes("verdict=") || l.includes("baseline: the real tree")).join("\n"));
+  writeFileSync(join(clone, "synthetic-openai-token.txt"), `sk-${"A".repeat(40)}\n`);
+  const leak = run(["bun", "run", gate, "--tree-only", "--no-local-store"]);
+  console.log("leak", leak.exit, leak.out.split("\n").filter((l) => l.startsWith("FAILING") || l.startsWith("  ") || l.startsWith("check-no-secrets:")).slice(-30).join("\n"));
+} finally {
+  rmSync(scratch, { recursive: true, force: true });
+}

+  const mergeSeed = encodeSeed(randomBytes(16), "secp256k1");
+  g(histRepo, "merge", "--no-ff", "--no-gpg-sign", "-m", `merge carries ${mergeSeed}`, "side");
+  const mergeSha = g(histRepo, "rev-parse", "HEAD").slice(0, 10);
+  const hr = scanRepo(histRepo, emptyStore, { tree: false, history: true });
+  console.log(`history rename-old-path=${hr.findings.some((f) => f.cls === "secret-file" && f.where.startsWith("state.json"))} annotated-tag=${hr.findings.some((f) => f.cls === "seed" && f.where.startsWith("tag "))} merge-message=${hr.findings.some((f) => f.cls === "seed" && f.where.startsWith(`commit ${mergeSha}`))}`);
+
+  console.log("\nSCRUBBER");
+  const unreported = `X${homedir()}/file`;
+  const scrubbed = scrub(unreported);
+  console.log(`non-detector substring: pre-path-hits=${pathHits(unreported).filter((h) => !h.allowedBy).length} changed=${scrubbed.text !== unreported}`);
+  const winFile = join(scratch, "windows.txt");
+  writeFileSync(winFile, "C:[synthetic personal-path redacted]/work\n");
+  const scrubRun = run(["bun", "run", join(import.meta.dir, "scrub-personal-data.ts"), winFile], ROOT);
+  console.log(`forward-slash Windows path scrubber exit=${scrubRun.exit}; ${scrubRun.out.trimEnd().split("\n").at(-1)}`);
+
+  console.log("\nUNTESTED MUTATION");
+  const clone = join(scratch, "mutant-repo");
+  const cloneRun = run(["git", "clone", "-q", "--no-hardlinks", ROOT, clone], ROOT);
+  if (cloneRun.exit !== 0) throw new Error(cloneRun.err);
+  mkdirSync(join(clone, "node_modules"));
+  for (const pkg of ["@noble", "ripple-address-codec", "ripple-keypairs"])
+    symlinkSync(join(ROOT, "node_modules", pkg), join(clone, "node_modules", pkg), "dir");
+  const mutantGate = join(clone, "tools", "check-no-secrets.ts");
+  const source = readFileSync(mutantGate, "utf8");
+  const openAiLine = '  { name: "OpenAI API key", re: /\\bsk-(?:proj-)?[A-Za-z0-9_-]{40,}/g },\n';
+  if (!source.includes(openAiLine)) throw new Error("OpenAI mutation did not apply");
+  writeFileSync(mutantGate, source.replace(openAiLine, ""));
+  const controls = run(["bun", "run", mutantGate, "--controls-only"], clone);
+  const harness = run(["bun", "run", join(clone, "tools", "break-no-secrets-gate.ts")], clone);
+  put(clone, "synthetic-openai-token.txt", `sk-${"A".repeat(40)}\n`);
+  const leakRun = run(["bun", "run", mutantGate, "--tree-only", "--no-local-store"], clone);
+  console.log(`deleted OpenAI detector controls: ${controls.out.trimEnd().split("\n").at(-1)}`);
+  console.log(`deleted OpenAI detector harness: ${harness.out.trimEnd().split("\n").at(-1)}`);
+  console.log(`deleted OpenAI detector planted token: ${leakRun.out.trimEnd().split("\n").at(-1)}`);
+} finally {
+  rmSync(scratch, { recursive: true, force: true });
+}

+import { mkdirSync, mkdtempSync, readFileSync, rmSync, symlinkSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
+import { tmpdir } from "node:os";
+import { join } from "node:path";
+
+const ROOT = join(import.meta.dir, "..");

+  ...process.env,

+  GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL: "/dev/null",

+  GIT_COMMITTER_EMAIL: "probe@example.invalid",
+};

+  const r = Bun.spawnSync(cmd, { cwd: clone, stdout: "pipe", stderr: "pipe", env });
+  return { exit: r.exitCode ?? -1, out: new TextDecoder().decode(r.stdout), err: new TextDecoder().decode(r.stderr) };

+import { randomBytes } from "node:crypto";
+import {
+  mkdirSync, mkdtempSync,
+  readFileSync, rmSync, symlinkSync, writeFileSync,
+} from "node:fs";
+import { homedir, tmpdir } from "node:os";
+import { dirname, join } from "node:path";
+import { encodeSeed } from "ripple-address-codec";
+import {
+  CITED_VECTORS, loadStore, pathHits, scanRepo, scanText,
+} from "./check-no-secrets.ts";
+import { scrub } from "./scrub-personal-data.ts";
+
+const ROOT = join(import.meta.dir, "..");
+const GATE = join(import.meta.dir, "check-no-secrets.ts");
+const scratch = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), "review056-"));
+const gitEnv = {
+  GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM: "1",
+  GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL: "/dev/null",
+  GIT_AUTHOR_NAME: "probe",
+  GIT_AUTHOR_EMAIL: "probe@example.invalid",
+  GIT_COMMITTER_NAME: "probe",
+  GIT_COMMITTER_EMAIL: "probe@example.invalid",
+};
+
+function run(cmd: string[], cwd = ROOT, input?: string, env = gitEnv) {
+  const r = Bun.spawnSync(cmd, { cwd, ...(input === undefined ? {} : { stdin: new TextEncoder().encode(input) }), stdout: "pipe", stderr: "pipe", env: { ...process.env, ...env } });
+  return {
+    exit: r.exitCode ?? -1,
+    out: new TextDecoder().decode(r.stdout),
+    err: new TextDecoder().decode(r.stderr),
+  };
+}
+
+function g(repo: string, ...args: string[]) {
+  const r = run(["git", ...args], repo);
+  if (r.exit !== 0) throw new Error(`git ${args.join(" ")} failed: ${r.err}`);
+  return r.out.trim();
+}
+
+function init(repo: string) {
+  mkdirSync(repo, { recursive: true });
+  g(repo, "init", "-q", "-b", "main");
+}
+
+function put(repo: string, rel: string, body: string) {
+  const p = join(repo, rel);
+  mkdirSync(dirname(p), { recursive: true });
+  writeFileSync(p, body);
+}
+
+function gate(args: string[]) {
+  const r = run(["bun", "run", GATE, ...args], ROOT);
+  const lines = r.out.trimEnd().split("\n");
+  return { exit: r.exit, last: lines.at(-1) ?? "", scanned: lines.find((l) => l.startsWith("scanned:")) ?? "" };
+}
+
+const emptyStore = { files: [], needles: [], secrets: 0, publicSkipped: 0 };
+const scalar64 = "a".repeat(64);
+const scalar63 = "a".repeat(63);
+
+try {
+  console.log("DETECTOR SHAPES");
+  const shapes: [string, string][] = [
+    ["63-digit labelled scalar", `private_key: ${scalar63}`],
+    ["pretty JSON scalar on next line", `{"private_key":\n  "${scalar64}"}`],
+    ["Markdown value before label", `| ${scalar64} | private key |`],
+    ["pretty JSON seed entropy on next line", `{"seed":\n  "${randomBytes(16).toString("hex")}"}`],
+    ["Windows forward-slash home", "C:[synthetic personal-path redacted]/work"],
+    ["AWS secret access key", `aws_secret_access_key=${"A".repeat(40)}`],
+  ];
+  for (const [name, text] of shapes) console.log(`${name}: findings=${scanText(text, emptyStore).filter((h) => !h.allowedBy).length}`);
+
+  const toy = `private_key: ${"0".repeat(63)}7`;
+  const publicSeed = encodeSeed(Buffer.alloc(16, 0x42), "ed25519");
+  const toyHits = scanText(toy, emptyStore);
+  const publicHits = scanText(`production_seed=${publicSeed}`, emptyStore);
+  console.log(`toy scalar: findings=${toyHits.filter((h) => !h.allowedBy).length} allowed=${toyHits.some((h) => h.allowedBy?.includes("toy scalar"))}`);
+  console.log(`repeated-byte production seed: findings=${publicHits.filter((h) => !h.allowedBy).length} allowed=${publicHits.some((h) => h.allowedBy?.includes("public entropy"))}`);
+
+  const storeDir = join(scratch, "malformed-store");
+  mkdirSync(storeDir);
+  const storedSeed = encodeSeed(randomBytes(16), "ed25519");
+  const omittedScalar = randomBytes(32).toString("hex");
+  writeFileSync(join(storeDir, "good.json"), JSON.stringify({ seed: storedSeed }));
+  writeFileSync(join(storeDir, "broken.json"), `{"sk":"${omittedScalar}"`);
+  const malformedStore = loadStore([storeDir], {});
+  console.log(`malformed store: secrets=${malformedStore.secrets} files=${malformedStore.files.length} bare-scalar-findings=${scanText(omittedScalar, malformedStore).filter((h) => !h.allowedBy).length}`);
+
+  console.log("\nENUMERATION");
+  const filterRepo = join(scratch, "filter-repo");
+  init(filterRepo);
+  const filteredSeed = encodeSeed(randomBytes(16), "ed25519");
+  put(filterRepo, ".gitattributes", "payload.txt filter=inject\n");
+  put(filterRepo, "payload.txt", "SAFE\n");
+  put(filterRepo, "cited.txt", CITED_VECTORS.map((v) => `private_key: ${v.value}`).join("\n") + "\n");
+  g(filterRepo, "config", "filter.inject.clean", `sed s/SAFE/${filteredSeed}/g`);
+  const beforeAdd = gate(["--repo", filterRepo, "--tree-only", "--no-local-store"]);
+  g(filterRepo, "add", "-A");
+  g(filterRepo, "commit", "-q", "--no-gpg-sign", "-m", "filtered");
+  const committedFiltered = g(filterRepo, "show", "HEAD:payload.txt").includes(filteredSeed);
+  const afterAdd = gate(["--repo", filterRepo, "--rev", "HEAD", "--no-local-store"]);
+  console.log(`clean filter before git add: ${beforeAdd.last}`);
+  console.log(`clean filter committed secret=${committedFiltered}; after commit: ${afterAdd.last}`);
+
+  const replaceRepo = join(scratch, "replace-repo");
+  init(replaceRepo);
+  const replacedSeed = encodeSeed(randomBytes(16), "secp256k1");
+  put(replaceRepo, "leak.txt", replacedSeed + "\n");
+  g(replaceRepo, "add", "-A");
+  g(replaceRepo, "commit", "-q", "--no-gpg-sign", "-m", "secret object");
+  const oldBlob = g(replaceRepo, "rev-parse", "HEAD:leak.txt");
+  const cleanBlob = run(["git", "hash-object", "-w", "--stdin"], replaceRepo, "SAFE\n").out.trim();
+  g(replaceRepo, "replace", oldBlob, cleanBlob);
+  writeFileSync(join(replaceRepo, "leak.txt"), "SAFE\n");
+  g(replaceRepo, "replace", "-d", oldBlob);
+  const original = g(replaceRepo, "cat-file", "blob", oldBlob).includes(replacedSeed);
+  g(replaceRepo, "replace", oldBlob, cleanBlob);
+  const revReplace = gate(["--repo", replaceRepo, "--rev", "HEAD", "--no-local-store"]);
+  const histReplace = gate(["--repo", replaceRepo, "--history-only", "--no-local-store"]);
+  console.log(`replace ref original secret=${original}; rev: ${revReplace.last}; history: ${histReplace.last}`);
+
+  const tabRepo = join(scratch, "tab-repo");
+  init(tabRepo);
+  put(tabRepo, "safe\t/Users/Alice/state.json", "SAFE\n");
+  g(tabRepo, "add", "-A");
+  g(tabRepo, "commit", "-q", "--no-gpg-sign", "-m", "odd path");
+  const rawPathHasSecretShape = g(tabRepo, "ls-tree", "-r", "--name-only", "HEAD").includes("state.json");
+  const tabRev = gate(["--repo", tabRepo, "--rev", "HEAD", "--no-local-store"]);
+  console.log(`tab filename contains secret path=${rawPathHasSecretShape}; rev: ${tabRev.last}`);
+
+  const sub = join(scratch, "sub");
+  init(sub);
+  put(sub, "ok.txt", "SAFE\n");
+  g(sub, "add", "-A");
+  g(sub, "commit", "-q", "--no-gpg-sign", "-m", "sub");
+  const superRepo = join(scratch, "super");
+  init(superRepo);
+  g(superRepo, "-c", "protocol.file.allow=always", "submodule", "add", "-q", sub, "vendor/sub");
+  g(superRepo, "commit", "-q", "--no-gpg-sign", "-am", "submodule");
+  const subRev = gate(["--repo", superRepo, "--rev", "HEAD", "--no-local-store"]);
+  console.log(`ordinary submodule rev: ${subRev.last}`);
+
+  const histRepo = join(scratch, "history-repo");
+  init(histRepo);
+  put(histRepo, "state.json", "{}\n");
+  g(histRepo, "add", "-A");
+  g(histRepo, "commit", "-q", "--no-gpg-sign", "-m", "old path");
+  g(histRepo, "mv", "state.json", "safe.txt");
+  g(histRepo, "commit", "-q", "--no-gpg-sign", "-m", "rename");
+  const tagSeed = encodeSeed(randomBytes(16), "ed25519");
+  g(histRepo, "tag", "-a", "probe-tag", "-m", `tag carries ${tagSeed}`);
+  g(histRepo, "checkout", "-q", "-b", "side", "HEAD~1");
+  put(histRepo, "side.txt", "SAFE\n");
+  g(histRepo, "add", "-A");
+  g(histRepo, "commit", "-q", "--no-gpg-sign", "-m", "side");
+  g(histRepo, "checkout", "-q", "main");
+  const mergeSeed = encodeSeed(randomBytes(16), "secp256k1");
+  g(histRepo, "merge", "--no-ff", "--no-gpg-sign", "-m", `merge carries ${mergeSeed}`, "side");
+  const mergeSha = g(histRepo, "rev-parse", "HEAD").slice(0, 10);
