+import { randomBytes } from "node:crypto";
+import {
+  mkdirSync, mkdtempSync,
+  readFileSync, rmSync, symlinkSync, writeFileSync,
+} from "node:fs";
+import { homedir, tmpdir } from "node:os";
+import { dirname, join } from "node:path";
+import { encodeSeed } from "ripple-address-codec";
+import {
+  CITED_VECTORS, loadStore, pathHits, scanRepo, scanText,
+} from "./check-no-secrets.ts";
+import { scrub } from "./scrub-personal-data.ts";
+
+const ROOT = join(import.meta.dir, "..");
+const GATE = join(import.meta.dir, "check-no-secrets.ts");
+const scratch = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), "review056-"));
+const gitEnv = {
+  GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM: "1",
+  GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL: "/dev/null",
+  GIT_AUTHOR_NAME: "probe",
+  GIT_AUTHOR_EMAIL: "probe@example.invalid",
+  GIT_COMMITTER_NAME: "probe",
+  GIT_COMMITTER_EMAIL: "probe@example.invalid",
+};
+
+function run(cmd: string[], cwd = ROOT, input?: string, env = gitEnv) {
+  const r = Bun.spawnSync(cmd, { cwd, ...(input === undefined ? {} : { stdin: new TextEncoder().encode(input) }), stdout: "pipe", stderr: "pipe", env: { ...process.env, ...env } });
+  return {
+    exit: r.exitCode ?? -1,
+    out: new TextDecoder().decode(r.stdout),
+    err: new TextDecoder().decode(r.stderr),
+  };
+}
+
+function g(repo: string, ...args: string[]) {
+  const r = run(["git", ...args], repo);
+  if (r.exit !== 0) throw new Error(`git ${args.join(" ")} failed: ${r.err}`);
+  return r.out.trim();
+}
+
+function init(repo: string) {
+  mkdirSync(repo, { recursive: true });
+  g(repo, "init", "-q", "-b", "main");
+}
+
+function put(repo: string, rel: string, body: string) {
+  const p = join(repo, rel);
+  mkdirSync(dirname(p), { recursive: true });
+  writeFileSync(p, body);
+}
+
+function gate(args: string[]) {
+  const r = run(["bun", "run", GATE, ...args], ROOT);
+  const lines = r.out.trimEnd().split("\n");
+  return { exit: r.exit, last: lines.at(-1) ?? "", scanned: lines.find((l) => l.startsWith("scanned:")) ?? "" };
+}
+
+const emptyStore = { files: [], needles: [], secrets: 0, publicSkipped: 0 };
+const scalar64 = "a".repeat(64);
+const scalar63 = "a".repeat(63);
+
+try {
+  console.log("DETECTOR SHAPES");
+  const shapes: [string, string][] = [
+    ["63-digit labelled scalar", `private_key: ${scalar63}`],
+    ["pretty JSON scalar on next line", `{"private_key":\n  "${scalar64}"}`],
+    ["Markdown value before label", `| ${scalar64} | private key |`],
+    ["pretty JSON seed entropy on next line", `{"seed":\n  "${randomBytes(16).toString("hex")}"}`],
+    ["Windows forward-slash home", "C:[synthetic personal-path redacted]/work"],
+    ["AWS secret access key", `aws_secret_access_key=${"A".repeat(40)}`],
+  ];
+  for (const [name, text] of shapes) console.log(`${name}: findings=${scanText(text, emptyStore).filter((h) => !h.allowedBy).length}`);
+
+  const toy = `private_key: ${"0".repeat(63)}7`;
+  const publicSeed = encodeSeed(Buffer.alloc(16, 0x42), "ed25519");
+  const toyHits = scanText(toy, emptyStore);
+  const publicHits = scanText(`production_seed=${publicSeed}`, emptyStore);
+  console.log(`toy scalar: findings=${toyHits.filter((h) => !h.allowedBy).length} allowed=${toyHits.some((h) => h.allowedBy?.includes("toy scalar"))}`);
+  console.log(`repeated-byte production seed: findings=${publicHits.filter((h) => !h.allowedBy).length} allowed=${publicHits.some((h) => h.allowedBy?.includes("public entropy"))}`);
+
+  const storeDir = join(scratch, "malformed-store");
+  mkdirSync(storeDir);
+  const storedSeed = encodeSeed(randomBytes(16), "ed25519");
+  const omittedScalar = randomBytes(32).toString("hex");
+  writeFileSync(join(storeDir, "good.json"), JSON.stringify({ seed: storedSeed }));
+  writeFileSync(join(storeDir, "broken.json"), `{"sk":"${omittedScalar}"`);
+  const malformedStore = loadStore([storeDir], {});
+  console.log(`malformed store: secrets=${malformedStore.secrets} files=${malformedStore.files.length} bare-scalar-findings=${scanText(omittedScalar, malformedStore).filter((h) => !h.allowedBy).length}`);
+
+  console.log("\nENUMERATION");
+  const filterRepo = join(scratch, "filter-repo");
+  init(filterRepo);
+  const filteredSeed = encodeSeed(randomBytes(16), "ed25519");
+  put(filterRepo, ".gitattributes", "payload.txt filter=inject\n");
+  put(filterRepo, "payload.txt", "SAFE\n");
+  put(filterRepo, "cited.txt", CITED_VECTORS.map((v) => `private_key: ${v.value}`).join("\n") + "\n");
+  g(filterRepo, "config", "filter.inject.clean", `sed s/SAFE/${filteredSeed}/g`);
+  const beforeAdd = gate(["--repo", filterRepo, "--tree-only", "--no-local-store"]);
+  g(filterRepo, "add", "-A");
+  g(filterRepo, "commit", "-q", "--no-gpg-sign", "-m", "filtered");
+  const committedFiltered = g(filterRepo, "show", "HEAD:payload.txt").includes(filteredSeed);
+  const afterAdd = gate(["--repo", filterRepo, "--rev", "HEAD", "--no-local-store"]);
+  console.log(`clean filter before git add: ${beforeAdd.last}`);
+  console.log(`clean filter committed secret=${committedFiltered}; after commit: ${afterAdd.last}`);
+
+  const replaceRepo = join(scratch, "replace-repo");
+  init(replaceRepo);
+  const replacedSeed = encodeSeed(randomBytes(16), "secp256k1");
+  put(replaceRepo, "leak.txt", replacedSeed + "\n");
+  g(replaceRepo, "add", "-A");
+  g(replaceRepo, "commit", "-q", "--no-gpg-sign", "-m", "secret object");
+  const oldBlob = g(replaceRepo, "rev-parse", "HEAD:leak.txt");
+  const cleanBlob = run(["git", "hash-object", "-w", "--stdin"], replaceRepo, "SAFE\n").out.trim();
+  g(replaceRepo, "replace", oldBlob, cleanBlob);
+  writeFileSync(join(replaceRepo, "leak.txt"), "SAFE\n");
+  g(replaceRepo, "replace", "-d", oldBlob);
+  const original = g(replaceRepo, "cat-file", "blob", oldBlob).includes(replacedSeed);
+  g(replaceRepo, "replace", oldBlob, cleanBlob);
+  const revReplace = gate(["--repo", replaceRepo, "--rev", "HEAD", "--no-local-store"]);
+  const histReplace = gate(["--repo", replaceRepo, "--history-only", "--no-local-store"]);
+  console.log(`replace ref original secret=${original}; rev: ${revReplace.last}; history: ${histReplace.last}`);
+
+  const tabRepo = join(scratch, "tab-repo");
+  init(tabRepo);
+  put(tabRepo, "safe\t/Users/Alice/state.json", "SAFE\n");
+  g(tabRepo, "add", "-A");
+  g(tabRepo, "commit", "-q", "--no-gpg-sign", "-m", "odd path");
+  const rawPathHasSecretShape = g(tabRepo, "ls-tree", "-r", "--name-only", "HEAD").includes("state.json");
+  const tabRev = gate(["--repo", tabRepo, "--rev", "HEAD", "--no-local-store"]);
+  console.log(`tab filename contains secret path=${rawPathHasSecretShape}; rev: ${tabRev.last}`);
+
+  const sub = join(scratch, "sub");
+  init(sub);
+  put(sub, "ok.txt", "SAFE\n");
+  g(sub, "add", "-A");
+  g(sub, "commit", "-q", "--no-gpg-sign", "-m", "sub");
+  const superRepo = join(scratch, "super");
+  init(superRepo);
+  g(superRepo, "-c", "protocol.file.allow=always", "submodule", "add", "-q", sub, "vendor/sub");
+  g(superRepo, "commit", "-q", "--no-gpg-sign", "-am", "submodule");
+  const subRev = gate(["--repo", superRepo, "--rev", "HEAD", "--no-local-store"]);
+  console.log(`ordinary submodule rev: ${subRev.last}`);
+
+  const histRepo = join(scratch, "history-repo");
+  init(histRepo);
+  put(histRepo, "state.json", "{}\n");
+  g(histRepo, "add", "-A");
+  g(histRepo, "commit", "-q", "--no-gpg-sign", "-m", "old path");
+  g(histRepo, "mv", "state.json", "safe.txt");
+  g(histRepo, "commit", "-q", "--no-gpg-sign", "-m", "rename");
+  const tagSeed = encodeSeed(randomBytes(16), "ed25519");
+  g(histRepo, "tag", "-a", "probe-tag", "-m", `tag carries ${tagSeed}`);
+  g(histRepo, "checkout", "-q", "-b", "side", "HEAD~1");
+  put(histRepo, "side.txt", "SAFE\n");
+  g(histRepo, "add", "-A");
+  g(histRepo, "commit", "-q", "--no-gpg-sign", "-m", "side");
+  g(histRepo, "checkout", "-q", "main");
+  const mergeSeed = encodeSeed(randomBytes(16), "secp256k1");
+  g(histRepo, "merge", "--no-ff", "--no-gpg-sign", "-m", `merge carries ${mergeSeed}`, "side");
+  const mergeSha = g(histRepo, "rev-parse", "HEAD").slice(0, 10);
+  const hr = scanRepo(histRepo, emptyStore, { tree: false, history: true });
+  console.log(`history rename-old-path=${hr.findings.some((f) => f.cls === "secret-file" && f.where.startsWith("state.json"))} annotated-tag=${hr.findings.some((f) => f.cls === "seed" && f.where.startsWith("tag "))} merge-message=${hr.findings.some((f) => f.cls === "seed" && f.where.startsWith(`commit ${mergeSha}`))}`);
+
+  console.log("\nSCRUBBER");
+  const unreported = `X${homedir()}/file`;
+  const scrubbed = scrub(unreported);
+  console.log(`non-detector substring: pre-path-hits=${pathHits(unreported).filter((h) => !h.allowedBy).length} changed=${scrubbed.text !== unreported}`);
+  const winFile = join(scratch, "windows.txt");
+  writeFileSync(winFile, "C:[synthetic personal-path redacted]/work\n");
+  const scrubRun = run(["bun", "run", join(import.meta.dir, "scrub-personal-data.ts"), winFile], ROOT);
+  console.log(`forward-slash Windows path scrubber exit=${scrubRun.exit}; ${scrubRun.out.trimEnd().split("\n").at(-1)}`);
+
+  console.log("\nUNTESTED MUTATION");
+  const clone = join(scratch, "mutant-repo");
+  const cloneRun = run(["git", "clone", "-q", "--no-hardlinks", ROOT, clone], ROOT);
+  if (cloneRun.exit !== 0) throw new Error(cloneRun.err);
+  mkdirSync(join(clone, "node_modules"));
+  for (const pkg of ["@noble", "ripple-address-codec", "ripple-keypairs"])
+    symlinkSync(join(ROOT, "node_modules", pkg), join(clone, "node_modules", pkg), "dir");
+  const mutantGate = join(clone, "tools", "check-no-secrets.ts");
+  const source = readFileSync(mutantGate, "utf8");
+  const openAiLine = '  { name: "OpenAI API key", re: /\\bsk-(?:proj-)?[A-Za-z0-9_-]{40,}/g },\n';
+  if (!source.includes(openAiLine)) throw new Error("OpenAI mutation did not apply");
+  writeFileSync(mutantGate, source.replace(openAiLine, ""));
+  const controls = run(["bun", "run", mutantGate, "--controls-only"], clone);
+  const harness = run(["bun", "run", join(clone, "tools", "break-no-secrets-gate.ts")], clone);
+  put(clone, "synthetic-openai-token.txt", `sk-${"A".repeat(40)}\n`);
+  const leakRun = run(["bun", "run", mutantGate, "--tree-only", "--no-local-store"], clone);
+  console.log(`deleted OpenAI detector controls: ${controls.out.trimEnd().split("\n").at(-1)}`);
+  console.log(`deleted OpenAI detector harness: ${harness.out.trimEnd().split("\n").at(-1)}`);
+  console.log(`deleted OpenAI detector planted token: ${leakRun.out.trimEnd().split("\n").at(-1)}`);
+} finally {
+  rmSync(scratch, { recursive: true, force: true });
+}

What this gate does NOT catch (each is an asserted-uncaught control):
  - a secret split across two string literals or two lines
  - a secret in UTF-16, base64, gzip or any other re-encoding
  - an unlabelled 64-hex ElGamal secret that is NOT in the local store (no shape rule can tell it from a hash)
  - a seed or key in RFC 1751 words, or as a decimal or byte array
  - a home path under a user name on the generic list
  - gitignored files, the reflog and unreachable objects: none of them is published
  - a hostname, a machine serial, or a person's name — only paths and email addresses are personal-data classes

pipeline_exit=0

   176	  // REVIEW-012b's leg proposed `OKM64 mod n`; the ruling chose `(OKM64 mod (n-1)) + 1`
   177	  // (REVIEW-012 §2.3, §6). Same public seeds, same OKM64, so these are public by
   178	  // construction. Checked 2026-10-18: each equals its printed OKM64 mod n.
   179	  { value: "f523da4f10344409a57e27ec6c2ff66e612962c4002ae347d53226fbca26925a", source: "REVIEW-012b leg's vector 1 sk_A, OKM64 mod n over the public counting-bytes secp256k1 seed" },
   180	  { value: "32afe79f8cc2d1745dee0ab5fbf2b6af39e1f63dc5b8d83c7200a05c8f56ee51", source: "REVIEW-012b leg's vector 2 sk_A, OKM64 mod n over the public counting-bytes ed25519 seed" },
   181	];
   182	
   183	/** A scalar this small is a toy test value: guessable, so not a secret anyone holds. */
   184	const TOY_SCALAR_BOUND = 1n << 64n;
   185	
   186	let publicScalarCache: Map<string, string> | null = null;
   187	/**
   188	 * Every scalar this repository could publish legitimately under a secret's
   189	 * label: the ElGamal key the ruled derivation gives each public seed, and the
   190	 * XRPL signing key `ripple-keypairs` gives it. Computed, never listed.
   191	 */
   192	export function publicScalars(): Map<string, string> {
   193	  if (publicScalarCache) return publicScalarCache;
   194	  const m = new Map<string, string>();
   195	  for (const rule of PUBLIC_ENTROPY) {
   196	    for (const e of rule.all()) {
   197	      for (const type of ["secp256k1", "ed25519"] as const) {
   198	        const seed = encodeSeed(Buffer.from(e), type);
   199	        const why = `derived from a ${type} seed whose entropy is ${rule.name}`;
   200	        m.set(deriveElGamalKey(seed).sk.toLowerCase(), `ElGamal key ${why}`);
   201	        const priv = deriveKeypair(seed).privateKey.toLowerCase();
   202	        m.set(priv, `signing key ${why}`);
   203	        m.set(priv.slice(2), `signing key ${why}`);
   204	      }
   205	    }
   206	  }
   207	  for (const v of CITED_VECTORS) m.set(v.value.toLowerCase(), v.source);
   208	  publicScalarCache = m;
   209	  return m;
   210	}

   212	// ------------------------------------------------------------------- findings

   214	export type Cls =
   215	  | "seed" | "seed-entropy-hex" | "labelled-scalar" | "known-value"
   270	/** Split an identifier into lower-case components on `_ . - $` and camelCase. */
   271	export function components(id: string): string[] {
   272	  return id
   273	    .replace(/([a-z0-9])([A-Z])/g, "$1 $2")
   274	    .split(/[\s_.\-$]+/)
   275	    .filter(Boolean)
   276	    .map((c) => c.toLowerCase());
   277	}
   278	const SECRET_COMPONENTS = new Set(["sk", "secret", "secretkey", "seckey", "private", "privatekey", "privkey", "priv"]);
   279	const ENTROPY_COMPONENTS = new Set(["seed", "entropy", "familyseed", "masterseed"]);
   280	const IDENT = /[A-Za-z_$][A-Za-z0-9_$.\-]*/g;
   281	const WINDOW = 40;
   282	
   283	function labelBefore(text: string, at: number, wanted: Set<string>): string | null {
   284	  const lineStart = text.lastIndexOf("\n", at - 1) + 1;
   285	  const win = text.slice(Math.max(lineStart, at - WINDOW), at);
   286	  for (const m of win.matchAll(IDENT)) if (components(m[0]).some((c) => wanted.has(c))) return m[0];
   287	  return null;
   288	}
   289	
   290	const HEX_64_66 = /(?<![0-9A-Za-z])(?:0x)?([0-9A-Fa-f]{66}|[0-9A-Fa-f]{64})(?![0-9A-Za-z])/g;
   291	const HEX_32 = /(?<![0-9A-Za-z])(?:0x)?([0-9A-Fa-f]{32})(?![0-9A-Za-z])/g;
   292	
   293	export function labelledHits(text: string): Hit[] {
   294	  const out: Hit[] = [];
   295	  const pub = publicScalars();
   296	  for (const m of text.matchAll(HEX_64_66)) {
   297	    const label = labelBefore(text, m.index!, SECRET_COMPONENTS);
   298	    if (!label) continue;
   299	    const v = m[1].toLowerCase();
   300	    const toy = BigInt("0x" + v) < TOY_SCALAR_BOUND ? "a toy scalar below 2^64" : undefined;
   301	    out.push({ cls: "labelled-scalar", at: m.index!, shown: redact(m[1]), why: `${m[1].length} hex digits after the label '${label}'`, allowedBy: pub.get(v) ?? toy });
   302	  }
   303	  for (const m of text.matchAll(HEX_32)) {
   304	    const label = labelBefore(text, m.index!, ENTROPY_COMPONENTS);
   305	    if (!label) continue;
   306	    const rule = publicEntropyRule(Uint8Array.from(Buffer.from(m[1], "hex")));
   307	    out.push({ cls: "seed-entropy-hex", at: m.index!, shown: redact(m[1]), why: `32 hex digits after the label '${label}'`, allowedBy: rule ? `public entropy: ${rule}` : undefined });
   308	  }
   309	  return out;
   310	}

   312	const CREDENTIALS: { name: string; re: RegExp }[] = [
   313	  { name: "PEM private key", re: /-----BEGIN (?:[A-Z0-9]+ )*PRIVATE KEY(?: BLOCK)?-----/g },
   314	  { name: "GitHub token", re: /\bgh[pousr]_[A-Za-z0-9]{36}\b/g },
   315	  { name: "GitHub fine-grained token", re: /\bgithub_pat_[A-Za-z0-9_]{50,}/g },
   430	export function defaultStorePaths(): string[] {
   431	  const p = [join(homedir(), ".wconf"), "/tmp/wconf"];
   432	  if (process.env.WCONF_STATE_BACKUP) p.unshift(process.env.WCONF_STATE_BACKUP);
   433	  return p;

   435	
   436	export function loadStore(paths: string[], env: Record<string, string | undefined> = process.env): Store {
   437	  const st: Store = { files: [], needles: [], secrets: 0, publicSkipped: 0 };
   438	  const files: string[] = [];
   439	  for (const p of paths) {
   440	    if (!existsSync(p)) continue;
   441	    const s = lstatSync(p);
   442	    if (s.isFile()) files.push(p);
   443	    else if (s.isDirectory()) for (const n of readdirSync(p)) if (n.includes(".json") && lstatSync(join(p, n)).isFile()) files.push(join(p, n));
   444	  }
   445	  // Counted by distinct VALUE: the same seed in two copies of state.json is one
   446	  // secret, and a count that doubled it would claim coverage it does not have.
   447	  const seen = new Set<string>();
   448	  const addSeed = (seed: string, label: string) => {
   449	    const d = decodeSeedLike(seed);
   450	    if (!d || !d.checksumOk) return;
   451	    if (publicEntropyRule(d.entropy)) { st.publicSkipped++; return; }
   452	    if (seen.has(seed)) return;
   453	    seen.add(seed);
   454	    st.secrets++;
   455	    st.needles.push(...needlesForSeed(seed, label));
   456	  };
   457	  const addHex = (v: string, label: string) => {
   458	    const k = v.toLowerCase();
   459	    if (seen.has(k)) return;
   460	    seen.add(k);
   461	    st.secrets++;
   462	    st.needles.push({ value: k, label, kind: "hex" });
   463	  };
   464	  for (const f of files) {
   465	    st.files.push(f);
   466	    const raw = readFileSync(f, "utf8");
   467	    const name = f.slice(f.lastIndexOf("/") + 1);
   468	    let json: unknown;
   469	    try { json = JSON.parse(raw); } catch { json = undefined; }
   470	    if (json === undefined) {
   471	      for (const m of raw.matchAll(B58RUN)) addSeed(m[0], `${name} (unparsed)`);
   472	      continue;
   473	    }
   474	    const walk = (o: unknown, path: string) => {
   475	      if (Array.isArray(o)) o.forEach((v, i) => walk(v, `${path}[${i}]`));
   476	      else if (o && typeof o === "object") for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(o)) walk(v, `${path}.${k}`);
   477	      else if (typeof o === "string") {
   478	        const key = path.slice(path.lastIndexOf(".") + 1).replace(/\[\d+\]$/, "");
   479	        if (decodeSeedLike(o)?.checksumOk) addSeed(o, `${name}:${path}`);
   480	        else if (/^[0-9A-Fa-f]{64}$/.test(o) && components(key).some((c) => SECRET_COMPONENTS.has(c))) addHex(o, `${name}:${path}`);
   481	      }
   482	    };
   483	    walk(json, "");
   484	  }
   485	  const envSk = env.WCONF_ELGAMAL_SK?.trim();
   515	export function scanText(text: string, store: Store): Hit[] {
   516	  return [
   517	    ...seedHits(text), ...labelledHits(text), ...credentialHits(text),
   518	    ...pathHits(text), ...emailHits(text), ...knownHits(text, store),
   519	  ];
   520	}

   524	function git(repo: string, args: string[], input?: Uint8Array, env?: Record<string, string>): Uint8Array {
   525	  const r = Bun.spawnSync(["git", "-C", repo, ...args], { stdin: input, stdout: "pipe", stderr: "pipe", env: env ? { ...process.env, ...env } : undefined });
   526	  if (r.exitCode !== 0) throw new Error(`git ${args.join(" ")} failed: ${new TextDecoder().decode(r.stderr).trim()}`);
   527	  return r.stdout;
   528	}
   529	const gitText = (repo: string, args: string[]) => new TextDecoder().decode(git(repo, args));
   530	
   531	/** Bytes as latin1, so every byte is one character and nothing is replaced. */
   532	const asText = (b: Uint8Array) => Buffer.from(b.buffer, b.byteOffset, b.byteLength).toString("latin1");
   533	
   534	export interface Unit { where: string; text: string; scope: "tree" | "history"; kind: "file" | "message" | "path" }
   535	
   536	function* batchObjects(repo: string, shas: string[]): Generator<{ sha: string; type: string; body: Uint8Array }> {
   537	  if (!shas.length) return;
   538	  const out = git(repo, ["cat-file", "--batch"], new TextEncoder().encode(shas.join("\n") + "\n"));
   539	  let i = 0;
   540	  const dec = new TextDecoder();
   541	  while (i < out.length) {
   542	    const nl = out.indexOf(10, i);
   543	    const [sha, type, size] = dec.decode(out.subarray(i, nl)).split(" ");
   544	    if (type === "missing") throw new Error(`object ${sha} missing`);
   545	    const n = Number(size);
   546	    yield { sha, type, body: out.subarray(nl + 1, nl + 1 + n) };
   547	    i = nl + 1 + n + 1;
   548	  }
   549	}
   550	
   551	/** What `git add -A` would commit, read from disk. Symlinks are not followed. */
   552	export function treeUnits(repo: string): Unit[] {
   553	  const names = gitText(repo, ["ls-files", "-z", "--cached", "--others", "--exclude-standard"]).split("\0").filter(Boolean);
   554	  const units: Unit[] = [];
   555	  for (const n of [...new Set(names)]) {
   556	    const p = join(repo, n);
   557	    units.push({ where: n, text: n, scope: "tree", kind: "path" });
   558	    if (!existsSync(p) && !lstatSync(p, { throwIfNoEntry: false })) {
   559	      // Deleted on disk, still in the index: the index copy is what a plain commit publishes.
   560	      try { units.push({ where: n, text: asText(git(repo, ["cat-file", "blob", `:${n}`])), scope: "tree", kind: "file" }); } catch { /* not in index either */ }
   561	      continue;
   562	    }
   563	    const s = lstatSync(p);
   564	    if (s.isSymbolicLink()) units.push({ where: `${n} (symlink target)`, text: readlinkSync(p), scope: "tree", kind: "file" });
   565	    else if (s.isFile()) units.push({ where: n, text: asText(readFileSync(p)), scope: "tree", kind: "file" });
   566	  }
   567	  return units;
   568	}
   569	
   570	/** Exactly the blobs of one commit's tree. */
   571	export function revUnits(repo: string, rev: string): Unit[] {
   572	  const entries = gitText(repo, ["ls-tree", "-r", "-z", "--full-tree", rev]).split("\0").filter(Boolean).map((l) => {
   573	    const [meta, path] = l.split("\t");
   574	    return { sha: meta.split(" ")[2], path };
   575	  });
   576	  const byPath = new Map(entries.map((e) => [e.sha, e.path] as const));
   577	  const units: Unit[] = entries.map((e) => ({ where: e.path, text: e.path, scope: "tree" as const, kind: "path" as const }));
   578	  for (const o of batchObjects(repo, [...new Set(entries.map((e) => e.sha))])) units.push({ where: byPath.get(o.sha)!, text: asText(o.body), scope: "tree", kind: "file" });
   579	  return units;
   580	}
   581	
   582	/** A commit or tag message, without the identity and signature headers. */
   583	function messageOf(body: Uint8Array): string {
   584	  const t = asText(body);
   585	  const split = t.indexOf("\n\n");
   586	  const head = split === -1 ? t : t.slice(0, split);
   587	  const msg = split === -1 ? "" : t.slice(split + 2);
   588	  // Keep header lines that are not identity or signature: none carry free text
   589	  // except `encoding`, so only the message is returned.
   590	  void head;
   591	  return msg;
   592	}
   593	
   594	export function historyUnits(repo: string): Unit[] {
   595	  const lines = gitText(repo, ["rev-list", "--objects", "--all"]).split("\n").filter(Boolean);
   596	  const pathOf = new Map<string, string>();
   597	  for (const l of lines) {
   598	    const sp = l.indexOf(" ");
   599	    const sha = sp === -1 ? l : l.slice(0, sp);
   600	    if (!pathOf.has(sha)) pathOf.set(sha, sp === -1 ? "" : l.slice(sp + 1));
   601	  }
   602	  const tags = gitText(repo, ["for-each-ref", "--format=%(objectname) %(objecttype)", "refs/tags"]).split("\n").filter((l) => l.endsWith(" tag")).map((l) => l.split(" ")[0]);
   603	  const units: Unit[] = [];
   604	  for (const o of batchObjects(repo, [...pathOf.keys(), ...tags])) {
   605	    const p = pathOf.get(o.sha) ?? "";
   606	    if (o.type === "blob") units.push({ where: `${p} @ blob ${o.sha.slice(0, 10)}`, text: asText(o.body), scope: "history", kind: "file" });
   607	    else if (o.type === "commit" || o.type === "tag") units.push({ where: `${o.type} ${o.sha.slice(0, 10)} message`, text: messageOf(o.body), scope: "history", kind: "message" });
   608	  }
   609	  const paths = new Set(gitText(repo, ["log", "--all", "-m", "--format=", "--name-only", "--no-renames"]).split("\n").filter(Boolean));
   610	  for (const p of paths) units.push({ where: `${p} (a path some commit touched)`, text: p, scope: "history", kind: "path" });
   611	  return units;
   612	}
   613	
   614	export interface Finding extends Hit { where: string; line: number; scope: "tree" | "history" }
   615	
   616	export function scanUnits(units: Unit[], store: Store): { findings: Finding[]; allowed: Map<string, number>; seenAllowed: Set<string> } {
   617	  const findings: Finding[] = [];
   618	  const allowed = new Map<string, number>();
   619	  const seenAllowed = new Set<string>();
   620	  for (const u of units) {
   621	    const hits = u.kind === "path" ? [secretFileHit(u.text), ...pathHits(u.text), ...emailHits(u.text)].filter((h): h is Hit => !!h) : scanText(u.text, store);
   622	    for (const h of hits) {
   623	      if (h.allowedBy) {
   624	        const k = `${h.cls}: ${h.allowedBy}`;
   625	        allowed.set(k, (allowed.get(k) ?? 0) + 1);
   840	    rmSync(root, { recursive: true, force: true });
   841	  }
   842	  return { run: controlsRun, failed: controlsFailed };
   843	}
   844	
   845	const dummy = (cls: Cls): Finding => ({ cls, at: 0, shown: "x", why: "x", where: "x", line: 1, scope: "tree" });
   846	
   847	// ------------------------------------------------------------- the decision
   848	
   849	export interface ScanResult { findings: Finding[]; allowed: Map<string, number>; seenAllowed: Set<string>; unitsTree: number; unitsHistory: number }
   850	
   851	export function scanRepo(repo: string, store: Store, o: { tree: boolean; history: boolean; rev?: string }): ScanResult {
   852	  const t = o.tree ? (o.rev ? revUnits(repo, o.rev) : treeUnits(repo)) : [];
   853	  const h = o.history ? historyUnits(repo) : [];
   854	  const r = scanUnits([...t, ...h], store);
   855	  return { ...r, unitsTree: t.filter((u) => u.kind !== "path").length, unitsHistory: h.filter((u) => u.kind !== "path").length };
   856	}
   857	
   858	interface DecideIn { findings: Finding[]; store: Store; requireStore: boolean; strictHistory: boolean; unitsTree: number; unitsHistory: number; tree: boolean; history: boolean }
   859	
   860	export function decide(d: DecideIn): { exit: 0 | 1 | 2; word: string; failing: Finding[]; reported: Finding[]; why?: string } {
   861	  if (d.requireStore && d.store.secrets === 0) return { exit: 2, word: "REFUSED", failing: [], reported: [], why: "no local secret store found; the known-value class cannot run (pass --no-local-store for the shape classes only)" };
   862	  if ((d.tree && d.unitsTree === 0) || (d.history && d.unitsHistory === 0)) return { exit: 2, word: "REFUSED", failing: [], reported: [], why: "zero files scanned; an empty scan is not a pass" };
   863	  const failing = d.findings.filter((f) => f.scope === "tree" || d.strictHistory || !PERSONAL.has(f.cls));
   864	  const reported = d.findings.filter((f) => !failing.includes(f));
   865	  if (failing.length) return { exit: 1, word: "FAIL", failing, reported };
   866	  return { exit: 0, word: d.requireStore ? "PASS" : "PASS-SHAPES-ONLY", failing, reported };
   867	}

   869	function printLimits() {
   870	  console.log("\nWhat this gate does NOT catch (each is an asserted-uncaught control):");
   871	  for (const l of LIMITS) console.log(`  - ${l}`);
   872	}
   873	
   874	function printFindings(title: string, fs: Finding[], cap = 25) {
   875	  if (!fs.length) return;
   876	  console.log(`\n${title}: ${fs.length}`);
   877	  const byCls = new Map<Cls, Finding[]>();
   878	  for (const f of fs) byCls.set(f.cls, [...(byCls.get(f.cls) ?? []), f]);
   879	  for (const [cls, list] of byCls) {
   880	    const files = new Map<string, number>();
   881	    for (const f of list) files.set(f.where, (files.get(f.where) ?? 0) + 1);
   882	    console.log(`  ${cls}: ${list.length} in ${files.size} location(s)`);
   883	    for (const f of list.slice(0, cap)) console.log(`    ${f.where}${f.line ? `:${f.line}` : ""}  ${f.why} — ${f.shown}`);
   884	    if (list.length > cap) console.log(`    … ${list.length - cap} more; per location:`), [...files].slice(0, 60).forEach(([w, n]) => console.log(`      ${n}  ${w}`));
   885	  }
   886	}

   888	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------- main
   889	
   890	if (import.meta.main) {
   891	  const argv = process.argv.slice(2);
   892	  const flag = (f: string) => argv.includes(f);
   893	  const opt = (f: string) => { const i = argv.indexOf(f); return i >= 0 ? argv[i + 1] : undefined; };
   894	  const finish = (word: string, exit: number) => { console.log(`check-no-secrets: verdict=${word} exit=${exit}`); process.exit(exit); };
   895	
   896	  const repo = realpathSync(opt("--repo") ?? join(import.meta.dir, ".."));
   897	  const rev = opt("--rev");
   898	  const requireStore = !flag("--no-local-store");
   899	  const doTree = !flag("--history-only");
   900	  const doHistory = !flag("--tree-only") && !rev;
   901	  const storePaths = argv.flatMap((a, i) => (a === "--store" ? [argv[i + 1]] : []));
   902	
   903	  console.log(`check-no-secrets over ${rev ? `the tree of ${rev}` : doTree ? "the publication set (what git add -A would commit)" : "nothing in the tree"}${doHistory ? " and all history" : ""}`);
   904	  const c = runControls();
   905	  console.log(`controls: ${c.run - c.failed.length}/${c.run} passed, run through the same scanRepo() as the real check`);
   906	  if (c.failed.length) {
   907	    for (const f of c.failed) console.log(`  CONTROL FAILED: ${f}`);
   908	    console.log("The detector is wrong; nothing it would report can be believed.");
   909	    finish("CONTROL-FAILED", 2);
   910	  }
   911	  // The fast self-check `break-no-secrets-gate.ts` drives: controls, and nothing else.
   912	  if (flag("--controls-only")) finish("CONTROLS-PASS", 0);
   913	
   914	  const store = requireStore ? loadStore(storePaths.length ? storePaths : defaultStorePaths()) : { files: [], needles: [], secrets: 0, publicSkipped: 0 };
   915	  if (requireStore) console.log(`known-value: ${store.secrets} secret(s) from ${store.files.length} file(s) in the local store, ${store.needles.length} encodings searched; ${store.publicSkipped} stored seed(s) skipped as public by construction`);
   916	  else console.log("known-value: NOT RUN (--no-local-store). An unlabelled ElGamal secret is invisible to this run. This is NOT the phase 1 gate.");
   917	
   918	  let r: ScanResult;
   919	  try { r = scanRepo(repo, store, { tree: doTree, history: doHistory, rev }); }
   920	  catch (e) { console.log(`REFUSED: ${String(e)}`); finish("REFUSED", 2); }
   921	  console.log(`scanned: ${r!.unitsTree} file(s) in the tree, ${r!.unitsHistory} history object(s)`);
   922	  console.log("allowed (each rule, and how many hits it permitted):");
   923	  for (const [k, n] of [...r!.allowed].sort()) console.log(`  ${n}  ${k}`);
   924	
   925	  if (doTree && !rev) {
   926	    const stale = CITED_VECTORS.filter((v) => !r!.seenAllowed.has(v.source));
   927	    if (stale.length) {
   928	      for (const s of stale) console.log(`  STALE CITED VECTOR: ${s.source} — no longer present; remove it from CITED_VECTORS`);
   929	      finish("CONTROL-FAILED", 2);
   930	    }
    70	
    71	/** Lines that report a failure, and only those. */
    72	function failureLines(r: Run): string[] {
    73	  const control = r.out.filter((l) => l.startsWith("  CONTROL FAILED:") || l.startsWith("  STALE CITED VECTOR:"));
    74	  const i = r.out.findIndex((l) => l.startsWith("FAILING:"));
    75	  if (i === -1) return control;
    76	  const end = r.out.findIndex((l, k) => k > i && (l.startsWith("REPORTED") || l.startsWith("What this gate")));
    77	  return [...control, ...r.out.slice(i, end === -1 ? undefined : end)];
    78	}

    80	interface Mutation { name: string; find: string; replace: string; needle: string; args?: string[]; expect?: "CONTROL-FAILED" }
    81	
    82	/** Apply one exact edit, asserting it matches exactly once. */
    83	function mutate(m: Mutation): string | null {
    84	  const n = SRC.split(m.find).length - 1;
    85	  return n === 1 ? SRC.replace(m.find, m.replace) : null;
    86	}

   146	function plantRun(p: Plant): Run {
   147	  const written: string[] = [];
   148	  let storeDir: string | undefined;
   149	  try {
   150	    for (const [rel, body] of Object.entries(p.files)) {
   151	      const abs = join(ROOT, rel);
   152	      if (existsSync(abs)) throw new Error(`refusing to overwrite ${rel}`);
   153	      // Remove the shallowest directory this plant creates, or the file itself.
   154	      let created = abs;
   155	      for (let d = dirname(abs); d !== ROOT && !existsSync(d); d = dirname(d)) created = d;
   205	  const selfTest = process.argv.includes("--self-test");
   206	  let n = 0, bad = 0;
   207	  const report = (name: string, want: Verdict | "REJECTED", got: { v: Verdict; detail: string }) => {
   208	    n++;
   209	    const ok = want === "REJECTED" ? got.v !== "TICK" : got.v === want;
   210	    if (!ok) bad++;
   211	    console.log(`${ok ? "✓" : "✗"} ${name}: ${got.v}${want === "REJECTED" ? " (must be rejected)" : ""} — ${got.detail}`);
   212	  };
   213	
   214	  if (selfTest) {
   215	    // Three probes that must be REJECTED and one that must tick, so a harness
   216	    // that rejects everything cannot pass its own self-test.
   217	    report("self-test: a no-op mutation must not count as caught", "REJECTED", judgeMutation({ name: "no-op", find: "const WINDOW = 40;", replace: "const WINDOW = 40; ", needle: "type annotation" }));
   218	    report("self-test: a crash must be classified CRASH", "REJECTED", judgeMutation({ name: "crash", find: "const WINDOW = 40;", replace: "const WINDOW = ;", needle: "type annotation" }));
   219	    report("self-test: the wrong control's failure must not count", "REJECTED", judgeMutation({ name: "wrong", find: "const WINDOW = 40;", replace: "const WINDOW = 4;", needle: "a GitHub token" }));
   220	    report("self-test: a correct probe must tick", "TICK", judgeMutation(MUTATIONS.find((m) => m.name === "JSON-escaped home rule removed")!));
   221	    finish(bad === 0, n, bad);
   222	  }
   223	
   224	  writeFileSync(MUTANT, SRC);
   225	  const base = run(MUTANT, ["--controls-only"]);
   226	  report("baseline: the unmutated copy passes its controls", "TICK", base.crash ? { v: "CRASH", detail: base.out.slice(-3).join(" | ") } : base.verdict === "CONTROLS-PASS" && base.exit === 0 ? { v: "TICK", detail: base.out.find((l) => l.startsWith("controls:")) ?? "" } : { v: "NOT-CAUGHT", detail: `verdict=${base.verdict}` });
   227	  for (const m of MUTATIONS) report(`mutation: ${m.name}`, "TICK", judgeMutation(m));
   228	
   229	  const treeBase = run(GATE, ["--tree-only"]);
   230	  const clean = !treeBase.crash && treeBase.verdict === "PASS" && treeBase.exit === 0;
   231	  report("baseline: the real tree passes before anything is planted", "TICK", clean ? { v: "TICK", detail: "PASS" } : { v: "NOT-CAUGHT", detail: `verdict=${treeBase.verdict} exit=${treeBase.exit}; tree plants cannot be judged against a failing baseline` });
   232	  if (clean) for (const p of plants()) report(`plant: ${p.name}`, "TICK", judgePlant(p));
   233	  else console.log("  (tree plants SKIPPED: a FAIL against a failing baseline proves nothing about the plant)");
   234	  finish(bad === 0, n, bad);
   235	}

    46	 * * **Print a value.** Counts per rule, per file, only.

    52	import { readFileSync, realpathSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
    53	import { homedir, tmpdir } from "node:os";
    54	import { join } from "node:path";
    55	import { emailHits, pathHits } from "./check-no-secrets.ts";
    56	
    57	const CODE = /\.(ts|tsx|js|mjs|cjs|rs|py|sh)$/;
    58	
    59	function substitutions(): { rule: string; from: string; to: string }[] {
    60	  const repo = realpathSync(join(import.meta.dir, ".."));
    61	  const home = realpathSync(homedir());
    62	  const subs: { rule: string; from: string; to: string }[] = [];
    63	  const t = realpathSync(tmpdir()).match(/^(\/private)?(\/var\/folders\/[^/]+\/[^/]+)/);
    64	  if (t) {
    65	    subs.push({ rule: "user-tmp", from: "/private" + t[2], to: "$USER_TMP" });
    66	    subs.push({ rule: "user-tmp", from: t[2], to: "$USER_TMP" });
    67	  }
    68	  const esc = (p: string) => p.replaceAll("/", "\\/");
    69	  const dash = (p: string) => p.replaceAll("/", "-");
    70	  subs.push(
    71	    { rule: "repo", from: esc(repo), to: "$REPO" },
    72	    { rule: "repo", from: repo, to: "$REPO" },
    73	    { rule: "repo-slug", from: dash(repo), to: "$REPO_SLUG" },
    74	    { rule: "home", from: esc(home), to: "$HOME" },
    75	    { rule: "home", from: home, to: "$HOME" },
    76	    { rule: "home-slug", from: dash(home) + "-", to: "$HOME_SLUG-" },
    77	  );
    78	  // Longest first, so the repository root wins over the home directory it is under.
    79	  return subs.sort((a, b) => b.from.length - a.from.length);
    80	}
    81	
    82	export function scrub(text: string): { text: string; counts: Map<string, number> } {
    83	  const counts = new Map<string, number>();
    84	  let out = text;
    85	  for (const s of substitutions()) {
    86	    const n = out.split(s.from).length - 1;
    87	    if (n) { counts.set(s.rule, (counts.get(s.rule) ?? 0) + n); out = out.replaceAll(s.from, s.to); }
    88	  }
    89	  const emails = emailHits(out).filter((h) => !h.allowedBy).sort((a, b) => b.at - a.at);
    90	  for (const h of emails) out = out.slice(0, h.at) + "redacted@example.invalid" + out.slice(h.at + h.len!);
    91	  if (emails.length) counts.set("email", emails.length);
    92	  return { text: out, counts };
    93	}

   100	  if (!files.length) { console.log("usage: scrub-personal-data.ts [--write] [--allow-code] <file>..."); process.exit(2); }
   101	  let left = 0, changed = 0;
   102	  const total = new Map<string, number>();
   103	  console.log("| file | substitutions |\n|---|---|");
   104	  for (const f of files) {
   105	    if (CODE.test(f) && !allowCode) { console.log(`| \`${f}\` | REFUSED: code file, fix the path by hand |`); left++; continue; }

    27	## What it found
    28	
    29	- **No secret, anywhere.** None of the 19 local secrets appears in the tree or in
    30	  history, in any encoding the gate searches. No credential shape appears either.
    31	- **No secret-store file was ever committed.** No commit on any ref ever touched
    32	  a path named `state.json`, `facility-state.json`, `facility-book.json`,
    33	  `profile.json`, `.env` or a key file. With the line above, this verifies the
    34	  plan's claim two ways rather than assuming it. Neither the path nor any value
    35	  from `/tmp/wconf/state.json` ever entered git.
    36	- **Every seed in the tree is public by construction.** Eleven checksum-valid
    37	  seeds appear, and the gate decoded each one. Each has entropy from a published
    38	  rule: one byte repeated, the bytes `0x00` to `0x0f`, or rippled's genesis
    39	  passphrase.
    40	- **Personal paths in 42 tracked files**: the home directory, the macOS
    41	  per-user temp root, and the repository's dash-encoded scratchpad name. 40 of
    42	  them are review transcripts and outputs, mostly Codex and Gemini legs that
    43	  printed their working directory. One is the F-12 evidence README. One was code,
    44	  a spike with the repository root hardcoded.
    45	- **Third-party email addresses in 4 review files**, one of which had no path.
    75	
    76	## What the scrub costs, measured
    77	
    78	- **Three recorded hashes no longer reproduce from the scrubbed text.**
    79	  `logs/06-runner-scripts.log` lists five runner scripts with their sha256.
    80	  Re-hashing each listing from the original log reproduces all five. From the
    81	  scrubbed log, `install-emsdk.sh` and `run-group.sh` still reproduce, and
    82	  `run-build.sh`, `run-test.sh` and `run-xtrace.sh` do not. Each of those three
    83	  had the home directory on its `NODE22=` line. The F-12 README says so where it
    84	  prints the scripts.
    85	- **No other recorded hash is affected.** The undefined-symbol set hashes in
    86	  `logs/08-gen-tables-and-symbol-sets.log` are over symbol names; that log's
    87	  only two changed lines are the paths in a `shasum` listing. The build-output
    88	  hashes are over binary outputs, and only the paths beside them changed.
    89	- **The originals are kept, locally, unpublished**, at
    90	  `docs/evidence/f12-emsdk-2026-10-18/logs-unscrubbed/`, which stays ignored. If
    91	  a negation or a `git add -f` ever brought them into the tree, the gate would fail
    92	  on them. `emsdk-logs-unscrubbed.sha256` and `emsdk-logs-scrubbed.sha256` record
    93	  both sides.
    94	
    95	## A decision this took, and why it is reversible
    96	
    97	`docs/reviews/` is a historical record, and the launch plan publishes it. 41
    98	review files had personal paths or emails redacted. **That changes the bytes of a
    99	record and not what any leg was shown to mean**: a path under the home directory is
   100	still a path under the home directory. The private archive keeps every original in
   101	history, which is not rewritten. The squash ruling (`CLAIMS.md` E-309) means history
   102	is not published. Vocabulary in `docs/reviews/` is untouched. Whether the
   103	`tapFailHard` translation reaches those files is still the owner's decision.
   104	
   105	## What this does not establish
   106	
   107	- **Personal data is still in history.** The gate reports 1,045 path hits and 30
   108	  email hits in old blobs, and does not fail on them, because history is squashed
   109	  at publication. If anyone proposes publishing the history,
   110	  `bun run tools/check-no-secrets.ts --strict-history` fails on them, and history
   111	  would need rewriting first.
   112	- **The gate is lexical, over named classes.** `check-no-secrets` prints what it
   113	  cannot see on every run. That covers a secret split across literals, any
   114	  re-encoding, an unlabelled scalar that is not in the local store, and hostnames
   115	  and names. Each limit is an asserted-uncaught control, so the list cannot go
   116	  stale silently.
   117	- **The publication commit has not been scanned, because it does not exist yet.**
   118	  Phase 1 item 5 creates it, and the gate's `--repo <clone> --rev <commit>` form
   119	  runs on exactly that tree. That run is the phase 1 gate, and this one is not.
   120	- **The gate is not externally reviewed yet.** The launch plan asks for one

   312	const CREDENTIALS: { name: string; re: RegExp }[] = [
   313	  { name: "PEM private key", re: /-----BEGIN (?:[A-Z0-9]+ )*PRIVATE KEY(?: BLOCK)?-----/g },
   314	  { name: "GitHub token", re: /\bgh[pousr]_[A-Za-z0-9]{36}\b/g },
   315	  { name: "GitHub fine-grained token", re: /\bgithub_pat_[A-Za-z0-9_]{50,}/g },
   316	  { name: "Anthropic API key", re: /\bsk-ant-[A-Za-z0-9_-]{20,}/g },
   317	  { name: "OpenAI API key", re: /\bsk-(?:proj-)?[A-Za-z0-9_-]{40,}/g },
   318	  { name: "AWS access key id", re: /\b(?:AKIA|ASIA)[0-9A-Z]{16}\b/g },
   319	  { name: "Slack token", re: /\bxox[abprs]-[A-Za-z0-9-]{10,}/g },
   320	  { name: "Google API key", re: /\bAIza[0-9A-Za-z_-]{35}\b/g },

   325	export function credentialHits(text: string): Hit[] {
   326	  const out: Hit[] = [];
   327	  for (const c of CREDENTIALS) for (const m of text.matchAll(c.re)) out.push({ cls: "credential", at: m.index!, shown: redact(m[0]), why: c.name });
   328	  return out;
   329	}

   892	  const flag = (f: string) => argv.includes(f);
   893	  const opt = (f: string) => { const i = argv.indexOf(f); return i >= 0 ? argv[i + 1] : undefined; };
   894	  const finish = (word: string, exit: number) => { console.log(`check-no-secrets: verdict=${word} exit=${exit}`); process.exit(exit); };
   895

   916	  else console.log("known-value: NOT RUN (--no-local-store). An unlabelled ElGamal secret is invisible to this run. This is NOT the phase 1 gate.");
   917

   919	  try { r = scanRepo(repo, store, { tree: doTree, history: doHistory, rev }); }
   920	  catch (e) { console.log(`REFUSED: ${String(e)}`); finish("REFUSED", 2); }
   921	  console.log(`scanned: ${r!.unitsTree} file(s) in the tree, ${r!.unitsHistory} history object(s)`);
   922	  console.log("allowed (each rule, and how many hits it permitted):");

   934	  if (d.why) console.log(`REFUSED: ${d.why}`);

    72	function failureLines(r: Run): string[] {
    73	  const control = r.out.filter((l) => l.startsWith("  CONTROL FAILED:") || l.startsWith("  STALE CITED VECTOR:"));
    74	  const i = r.out.findIndex((l) => l.startsWith("FAILING:"));
    75	  if (i === -1) return control;
    76	  const end = r.out.findIndex((l, k) => k > i && (l.startsWith("REPORTED") || l.startsWith("What this gate")));
    77	  return [...control, ...r.out.slice(i, end === -1 ? undefined : end)];
    78	}
    79	
    80	interface Mutation { name: string; find: string; replace: string; needle: string; args?: string[]; expect?: "CONTROL-FAILED" }
    81	
    82	/** Apply one exact edit, asserting it matches exactly once. */
    83	function mutate(m: Mutation): string | null {
    84	  const n = SRC.split(m.find).length - 1;
    85	  return n === 1 ? SRC.replace(m.find, m.replace) : null;
    86	}

   111	  // so it does not crash on a dangling link and trip the wrong control.
   112	  { name: "symlinks followed", find: "if (s.isSymbolicLink()) units.push({ where: `${n} (symlink target)`, text: readlinkSync(p), scope: \"tree\", kind: \"file\" });", replace: "if (s.isSymbolicLink()) { units.push({ where: `${n} (symlink target)`, text: readlinkSync(p), scope: \"tree\", kind: \"file\" }); if (existsSync(p)) units.push({ where: n, text: asText(readFileSync(p)), scope: \"tree\", kind: \"file\" }); }", needle: "does not follow a symlink" },

   132	interface Plant { name: string; files: Record<string, string>; store?: Record<string, string>; expect: "FAIL" | "PASS"; needle?: string }
   133	
   134	function plants(): Plant[] {
   135	  const tag = `zz-break-${process.pid}`;
   136	  const sk = randomBytes(32).toString("hex");
   137	  return [
   138	    { name: "a real-entropy seed in an untracked file", files: { [`${tag}.txt`]: `seed: ${encodeSeed(randomBytes(16), "ed25519")}\n` }, expect: "FAIL", needle: `${tag}.txt` },
   139	    { name: "an ElGamal secret under docs/reviews", files: { [`docs/reviews/${tag}.md`]: `{"sk": "${randomBytes(32).toString("hex")}"}\n` }, expect: "FAIL", needle: `docs/reviews/${tag}.md` },
   140	    { name: "a stored secret, bare, against a synthetic store", files: { [`${tag}-known.txt`]: `${sk}\n` }, store: { "state.json": JSON.stringify({ x: { sk } }) }, expect: "FAIL", needle: `${tag}-known.txt` },
   141	    { name: "this machine's home path in an evidence README", files: { [`docs/evidence/${tag}/README.md`]: `ran from ${homedir()}/x\n` }, expect: "FAIL", needle: `docs/evidence/${tag}/README.md` },
   142	    { name: "a seed in a gitignored file still PASSES", files: { [`${tag}.log`]: `${encodeSeed(randomBytes(16), "secp256k1")}\n` }, expect: "PASS" },
   143	  ];
   144	}

   146	function plantRun(p: Plant): Run {
   147	  const written: string[] = [];
   148	  let storeDir: string | undefined;
   149	  try {
   150	    for (const [rel, body] of Object.entries(p.files)) {
   215	    // Three probes that must be REJECTED and one that must tick, so a harness
   216	    // that rejects everything cannot pass its own self-test.
   217	    report("self-test: a no-op mutation must not count as caught", "REJECTED", judgeMutation({ name: "no-op", find: "const WINDOW = 40;", replace: "const WINDOW = 40; ", needle: "type annotation" }));
   218	    report("self-test: a crash must be classified CRASH", "REJECTED", judgeMutation({ name: "crash", find: "const WINDOW = 40;", replace: "const WINDOW = ;", needle: "type annotation" }));
   219	    report("self-test: the wrong control's failure must not count", "REJECTED", judgeMutation({ name: "wrong", find: "const WINDOW = 40;", replace: "const WINDOW = 4;", needle: "a GitHub token" }));
   220	    report("self-test: a correct probe must tick", "TICK", judgeMutation(MUTATIONS.find((m) => m.name === "JSON-escaped home rule removed")!));
   221	    finish(bad === 0, n, bad);
   222	  }
   223	
   224	  writeFileSync(MUTANT, SRC);
   225	  const base = run(MUTANT, ["--controls-only"]);
   226	  report("baseline: the unmutated copy passes its controls", "TICK", base.crash ? { v: "CRASH", detail: base.out.slice(-3).join(" | ") } : base.verdict === "CONTROLS-PASS" && base.exit === 0 ? { v: "TICK", detail: base.out.find((l) => l.startsWith("controls:")) ?? "" } : { v: "NOT-CAUGHT", detail: `verdict=${base.verdict}` });
   227	  for (const m of MUTATIONS) report(`mutation: ${m.name}`, "TICK", judgeMutation(m));
   228	
   229	  const treeBase = run(GATE, ["--tree-only"]);
   230	  const clean = !treeBase.crash && treeBase.verdict === "PASS" && treeBase.exit === 0;
   231	  report("baseline: the real tree passes before anything is planted", "TICK", clean ? { v: "TICK", detail: "PASS" } : { v: "NOT-CAUGHT", detail: `verdict=${treeBase.verdict} exit=${treeBase.exit}; tree plants cannot be judged against a failing baseline` });
   232	  if (clean) for (const p of plants()) report(`plant: ${p.name}`, "TICK", judgePlant(p));
   233	  else console.log("  (tree plants SKIPPED: a FAIL against a failing baseline proves nothing about the plant)");
   234	  finish(bad === 0, n, bad);
   235	}
    45	 *   are refused unless `--allow-code` is given.
    46	 * * **Print a value.** Counts per rule, per file, only.
    47	 * * **Touch a secret.** Seeds, scalars and credentials are not personal data and

    52	import { readFileSync, realpathSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
    53	import { homedir, tmpdir } from "node:os";
    54	import { join } from "node:path";
    55	import { emailHits, pathHits } from "./check-no-secrets.ts";
    56	
    57	const CODE = /\.(ts|tsx|js|mjs|cjs|rs|py|sh)$/;
    58	
    59	function substitutions(): { rule: string; from: string; to: string }[] {
    60	  const repo = realpathSync(join(import.meta.dir, ".."));
    61	  const home = realpathSync(homedir());
    62	  const subs: { rule: string; from: string; to: string }[] = [];
    63	  const t = realpathSync(tmpdir()).match(/^(\/private)?(\/var\/folders\/[^/]+\/[^/]+)/);
    64	  if (t) {
    65	    subs.push({ rule: "user-tmp", from: "/private" + t[2], to: "$USER_TMP" });
    66	    subs.push({ rule: "user-tmp", from: t[2], to: "$USER_TMP" });
    67	  }
    68	  const esc = (p: string) => p.replaceAll("/", "\\/");
    69	  const dash = (p: string) => p.replaceAll("/", "-");
    70	  subs.push(
    71	    { rule: "repo", from: esc(repo), to: "$REPO" },
    72	    { rule: "repo", from: repo, to: "$REPO" },
    73	    { rule: "repo-slug", from: dash(repo), to: "$REPO_SLUG" },
    74	    { rule: "home", from: esc(home), to: "$HOME" },
    75	    { rule: "home", from: home, to: "$HOME" },
    76	    { rule: "home-slug", from: dash(home) + "-", to: "$HOME_SLUG-" },
    77	  );
    78	  // Longest first, so the repository root wins over the home directory it is under.
    79	  return subs.sort((a, b) => b.from.length - a.from.length);
    80	}
    81	
    82	export function scrub(text: string): { text: string; counts: Map<string, number> } {
    83	  const counts = new Map<string, number>();
    84	  let out = text;
    85	  for (const s of substitutions()) {
    86	    const n = out.split(s.from).length - 1;
    87	    if (n) { counts.set(s.rule, (counts.get(s.rule) ?? 0) + n); out = out.replaceAll(s.from, s.to); }
    88	  }
    89	  const emails = emailHits(out).filter((h) => !h.allowedBy).sort((a, b) => b.at - a.at);
    90	  for (const h of emails) out = out.slice(0, h.at) + "redacted@example.invalid" + out.slice(h.at + h.len!);
    91	  if (emails.length) counts.set("email", emails.length);
    92	  return { text: out, counts };
    93	}

   100	  if (!files.length) { console.log("usage: scrub-personal-data.ts [--write] [--allow-code] <file>..."); process.exit(2); }
   101	  let left = 0, changed = 0;

## Seam 1 — Controls and mutation harness

### S1-1 — HIGH — An existing credential detector can be deleted while every control and all 40 harness probes pass

At [check-no-secrets.ts:312]($USER_TMP/T/x.WSd2dt/repo/tools/check-no-secrets.ts:312), ten credential patterns exist, but controls exercise only GitHub and PEM. The 33 mutations at [break-no-secrets-gate.ts:88]($USER_TMP/T/x.WSd2dt/repo/tools/break-no-secrets-gate.ts:88) likewise mutate only those two patterns.

Mutation: delete the OpenAI-key entry at line 317. Plant: `sk-` followed by 40 `A` characters.

Wrong output:

```text
deleted OpenAI detector controls: check-no-secrets: verdict=CONTROLS-PASS exit=0
deleted OpenAI detector harness: break-no-secrets-gate: verdict=PASS probes=40 failed=0
deleted OpenAI detector planted token: check-no-secrets: verdict=PASS-SHAPES-ONLY exit=0
```

Command run:

```sh
bun run tools/.review056-mutation.ts
```

That was a temporary untracked mutation driver and was removed afterward.

The existing 33 mutations are mostly credible one-edit regressions; several regex removals are mechanically artificial but exercise the intended failure. The principal problem is coverage: most credential patterns and the entire `--rev` enumerator have no mutation probe.

## Seam 2 — Enumeration

### S2-1 — HIGH — `.gitattributes` clean filters can inject a secret after tree scanning

[treeUnits()]($USER_TMP/T/x.WSd2dt/repo/tools/check-no-secrets.ts:551) reads working-tree bytes directly, but `git add -A` applies clean filters before creating blobs.

Synthetic input:

```gitattributes
payload.txt filter=inject
```

`payload.txt` contained `SAFE`; the configured clean filter replaced it with a generated XRPL seed.

Wrong output:

```text
clean filter before git add: check-no-secrets: verdict=PASS-SHAPES-ONLY exit=0
clean filter committed secret=true; after commit: check-no-secrets: verdict=FAIL exit=1
```

Thus the gate passed the tree immediately before `git add -A`, although `git add -A` committed a seed.

Command run:

```sh
bun run tools/.review056-probes.ts
```

The temporary probe driver was removed.

### S2-2 — HIGH — Git replacement objects hide secrets from both revision and history scans

Git commands at [check-no-secrets.ts:524]($USER_TMP/T/x.WSd2dt/repo/tools/check-no-secrets.ts:524) do not set `GIT_NO_REPLACE_OBJECTS`. Consequently, `git cat-file --batch` at lines 536–546 transparently substitutes objects named by `refs/replace`.

Fixture: commit a random XRPL seed, replace its blob locally with `SAFE`, and overwrite the worktree with `SAFE`. The original commit still references the secret-bearing object and that is what would normally be pushed.

Wrong output:

```text
replace ref original secret=true
rev: check-no-secrets: verdict=PASS-SHAPES-ONLY exit=0
history: check-no-secrets: verdict=PASS-SHAPES-ONLY exit=0
```

Command run:

```sh
bun run tools/.review056-probes.ts
```

### S2-3 — MEDIUM — A tab in a committed filename hides its suffix in `--rev`

[revUnits()]($USER_TMP/T/x.WSd2dt/repo/tools/check-no-secrets.ts:570) parses `ls-tree -z` records with `l.split("\t")`. A path containing another tab is truncated.

Concrete committed path:

```text
safe<TAB>[synthetic personal-path redacted]/state.json
```

Git retained the whole filename, including both a personal path and `state.json`; the gate scanned only `safe`.

Wrong output:

```text
tab filename contains secret path=true
rev: check-no-secrets: verdict=PASS-SHAPES-ONLY exit=0
```

Command run:

```sh
bun run tools/.review056-probes.ts
```

### S2-4 — LOW — Ordinary submodules make `--rev` refuse

`revUnits()` sends gitlink object IDs to `cat-file`; a normal superproject does not contain the submodule’s commit object. [batchObjects()]($USER_TMP/T/x.WSd2dt/repo/tools/check-no-secrets.ts:536) therefore throws “object … missing”.

Wrong output:

```text
ordinary submodule rev: check-no-secrets: verdict=REFUSED exit=2
```

Command run:

```sh
bun run tools/.review056-probes.ts
```

This is a false fail, not a secret-publishing pass.

No additional enumeration findings: the synthetic history fixture successfully detected an old renamed `state.json` path, a seed in an annotated-tag message, and a seed in a merge-commit message. Existing controls correctly cover ordinary disk/index deletion differences and symlinks.

## Seam 3 — Detector holes and scrubber

### S3-1 — HIGH — Pretty JSON, output-first tables, and unpadded private scalars pass

[labelBefore()]($USER_TMP/T/x.WSd2dt/repo/tools/check-no-secrets.ts:283) searches only backward, on the same line, within 40 characters. `HEX_64_66` at line 290 recognizes only exactly 64 or 66 digits.

All these returned zero findings:

```text
{"private_key":
 "aaaaaaaa…(64 digits)"}

| aaaaaaaa…(64 digits) | private key |

private_key: aaaaaaaa…(63 digits)
```

The 63-digit value is a valid unpadded scalar shape; approximately 1/16 of uniformly generated scalars begin with a zero nibble when rendered without padding.

Command and output:

```sh
bun -e 'import {scanText} from "./tools/check-no-secrets.ts"; /* five cases */'
```

```text
63-digit 0
pretty-json 0
markdown-reversed 0
pretty-entropy 0
aws-secret 0
```

Concrete input:

```text
aws_secret_access_key=AAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAA
```

Wrong output: `scanText` returned zero findings.

Command: the same `bun -e` detector command above, producing `aws-secret 0`.

### S3-3 — HIGH — A malformed store silently omits its unlabelled scalar

At [check-no-secrets.ts:468]($USER_TMP/T/x.WSd2dt/repo/tools/check-no-secrets.ts:468), malformed JSON falls back to scanning only base58 seeds. A 64-hex scalar in that store is silently lost. If another valid store entry exists, the non-empty-store refusal does not activate.

Fixture:

- `good.json`: one random valid seed.
- `broken.json`: truncated `{"sk":"<64 hex>"`.
- Scan the same scalar bare in publication text.

Output:

```text
files=2 secrets=1 bareScalarFindings=0
```

Command run:

```sh
bun -e 'import {loadStore,scanText} from "./tools/check-no-secrets.ts"; /* creates and removes synthetic store */'
```

This defeats the only class capable of recognizing an unlabelled local scalar.

### S3-4 — LOW — The scrubber both over-replaces and can leave an “other user” path unreported

The substitutions at [scrub-personal-data.ts:82]($USER_TMP/T/x.WSd2dt/repo/tools/scrub-personal-data.ts:82) use unrestricted `replaceAll`.

- `X + homedir() + /file` had zero `pathHits`, but `scrub()` changed it anyway.
- `/home/user/private-client/work` remained unchanged and had zero failing hits because `user` is generic, contrary to the scrubber’s statement that another user’s remaining home is printed.

## Seam 4 — Allow rules

## Seam 5 — Decision layer and record

### S5-1 — MEDIUM — Contradictory mode flags scan zero files and return PASS

Argument selection at [check-no-secrets.ts:899]($USER_TMP/T/x.WSd2dt/repo/tools/check-no-secrets.ts:899) can set both `doTree` and `doHistory` false. The zero-scan refusal at line 862 checks only enabled scopes, so neither side fires.

Command and wrong output:

```sh

  rg '^(known-value:|scanned:|check-no-secrets:)'
```


`revUnits()` deduplicates entries by object ID at [lines 576–578]($USER_TMP/T/x.WSd2dt/repo/tools/check-no-secrets.ts:576), and `scanRepo()` labels the resulting number as files. Current `HEAD` has 616 blob paths but `--rev HEAD` reports 613 files.

```sh

```

Outputs: `616` versus `scanned: 613 file(s)`.

The evidence block at [secret-sweep README:18]($USER_TMP/T/x.WSd2dt/repo/docs/evidence/secret-sweep-2026-10-18/README.md:18) calls its 613-file measurement the “final tree”, but commit `aaa95f2` contains 616 blob paths. At least three final-tree paths were added after that run.
